Existing alignment methods, including Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) and its differentiable variants, rely on deterministic similarity measures and are therefore sensitive to heterogeneous and noisy features. In this work, we introduce uncertainty-aware alignment, a probabilistic framework that models pairwise correspondences with heteroscedastic uncertainty and performs structured matching along alignment paths. Our formulation, uncertainty-DTW (uDTW), assigns each correspondence a Normal distribution and parametrizes each alignment path by a Maximum Likelihood Estimate objective consisting of (i) a precision-weighted matching term that suppresses unreliable features, and (ii) a log-variance regularization that prevents degenerate solutions. This yields a probabilistic alignment mechanism that is robust to noise and interpretable, as uncertainty directly reflects the reliability of matches. We further generalize this framework from temporal sequences to tokenized visual representations, enabling structured matching over sets of visual tokens. The learned uncertainty can be interpreted as a reverse-attention: semantically relevant regions exhibit low uncertainty and dominate the alignment, while ambiguous/noisy regions have high uncertainty. This provides a connection between alignment, attention, and uncertainty modeling. We evaluate the proposed framework across diverse domains. The results demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods and show that learned uncertainty correlates with semantic importance. These findings establish uncertainty-aware alignment as a general, robust, and interpretable framework for learning from structured data.

中文摘要翻译: 对齐结构化数据是计算机视觉和机器学习中的一个基本问题，支撑着时间序列分析、人体动作识别和视觉表征学习等任务。现有的对齐方法，包括动态时间规整（DTW）及其可微变体，依赖于确定性的相似度度量，因此对异质性和噪声特征敏感。在这项工作中，我们引入了不确定性感知对齐（uncertainty-aware alignment），这是一个概率框架，它用异方差不确定性建模成对对应关系，并沿着对齐路径执行结构化匹配。我们的公式——不确定性DTW（uDTW）——为每个对应关系分配一个正态分布，并通过一个最大似然估计目标函数来参数化每条对齐路径，该目标函数包含：(i) 一个精度加权匹配项，用于抑制不可靠的特征，以及(ii) 一个对数方差正则化项，用于防止退化解。这产生了一种对噪声鲁棒且可解释的概率对齐机制，因为不确定性直接反映了匹配的可靠性。我们进一步将该框架从时序序列推广到token化的视觉表征，使得能够对视觉token集进行结构化匹配。学习到的不确定性可以被解释为一种反向注意力：语义相关区域表现出低不确定性并主导对齐，而模糊/噪声区域表现出高不确定性。这建立了对齐、注意力和不确定性建模之间的联系。我们在不同领域评估了所提出的框架。结果表明，该方法一致性地优于最先进的方法，并且学习到的不确定性与语义重要性相关。这些发现确立了不确定性感知对齐作为从结构化数据进行学习的通用、鲁棒且可解释的框架。

</details>


### [91] [Trust-Aware Joint Feature-Prediction Discrepancy for Robust Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25119)
*Xi Ding,Lei Wang,Syuan-Hao Li,Yongsheng Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出信任感知领域自适应框架，通过联合特征-预测差异（JFPD）在特征和预测空间中建模领域差异，并引入样本级信任机制（不确定性感知信任和语义对齐信任）来加权差异估计，从而在领域偏移下实现更可靠的迁移。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有领域自适应方法仅从特征空间或预测空间的单一视角估计领域差异，忽视了在领域偏移下特征表示和语义预测都可能变得不可靠的问题，对所有目标样本等同对待会导致误导性对齐和次优迁移。

Method: 提出信任感知领域自适应框架，核心是联合特征-预测差异（JFPD），该公式联合捕获特征表征散度和预测散度，并通过两种互补的信任机制加权：不确定性感知信任（基于预测熵抑制不可靠预测）和语义对齐信任（基于特征空间中原型相似性强调良好对齐的表示）。JFPD优先考虑置信度高且语义一致的样本，降低噪声或模糊样本权重，提供可靠性感知的领域差异估计，并集成到训练目标中引导自适应向目标域的可靠区域进行。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上，所提框架持续取得优越的自适应性能，且其差异估计与目标域误差具有相关性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次揭示了在领域自适应中建模特征与预测之间信任交互的重要性。

Original Abstract: Domain adaptation aims to mitigate performance degradation caused by distribution shifts between a labeled source domain and an unlabeled or sparsely labeled target domain. Most existing approaches estimate domain discrepancy either in feature space or in prediction space. However, these single-perspective strategies overlook a critical problem under domain shift: the reliability of the signals used for alignment. In practice, both learned representations and semantic predictions may become unreliable, and treating all target samples equally can lead to misleading alignment and suboptimal transfer. We introduce trust-aware domain adaptation, a principled framework that models domain discrepancy through the reliability of feature and prediction signals. Central to our approach is the Joint Feature-Prediction Discrepancy (JFPD), a unified formulation that jointly captures representation divergence and prediction divergence while weighting their contributions by sample-specific trust. Trust is quantified via two complementary mechanisms: uncertainty-aware trust, derived from prediction entropy to suppress unreliable predictions, and semantic-alignment trust, computed from prototype similarity in feature space to emphasize well-aligned representations. By prioritizing confident and semantically consistent samples while down-weighting noisy or ambiguous ones, JFPD provides a reliability-aware estimate of domain discrepancy. We further integrate JFPD into a training objective that guides adaptation toward trustworthy regions of the target domain. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently achieves superior adaptation performance and yields discrepancy estimates that correlate with target-domain error. This work addresses, for the first time, the importance of modeling trust in the interaction between features and predictions for domain adaptation.

中文摘要翻译: 领域自适应旨在缓解由有标签源域与无标签或稀疏标签目标域之间的分布偏移引起的性能退化。大多数现有方法在特征空间或预测空间中估计领域差异。然而，这些单一视角的策略忽视了领域偏移下的一个关键问题：用于对齐的信号的可信度。在实践中，学习到的表示和语义预测都可能变得不可靠，对所有目标样本等同对待会导致误导性对齐和次优迁移。我们引入信任感知领域自适应，这是一个通过特征和预测信号的可信度来建模领域差异的原理性框架。我们方法的核心是联合特征-预测差异（JFPD），这是一种统一的公式，它联合捕获表征散度和预测散度，同时通过样本级信任加权它们的贡献。信任通过两种互补机制量化：不确定性感知信任，基于预测熵推导以抑制不可靠的预测；以及语义对齐信任，基于特征空间中的原型相似性计算以强调良好对齐的表示。通过优先考虑置信度高且语义一致的样本，同时降低噪声或模糊样本的权重，JFPD提供了可靠性感知的领域差异估计。我们进一步将JFPD集成到一个训练目标中，引导自适应向目标域的可靠区域进行。在标准基准上的实验表明，所提出的框架持续取得了优越的自适应性能，并且其差异估计与目标域误差相关。该工作首次揭示了在领域自适应中建模特征与预测之间信任交互的重要性。

</details>


### [92] [Methodology for Creating a Clinically Verified Dermoscopic Image Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25168)
*Kozachok Elena Sergeevna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套用于构建临床验证的皮肤镜影像数据集的方法论，包含标准化采集流程、结构化元数据模型和多阶段专家诊断验证，并基于此构建了一个包含1026张图像、涵盖9种疾病类别、所有恶性病变均经组织学验证的临床验证数据集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自动化诊断支持系统的性能不仅依赖于图像数量，还依赖于图像采集的可重复性、结构化元数据的完整性和诊断标签的可靠性。现有国际数据集主要在不同于俄罗斯门诊实践和移动皮肤镜的条件下创建，缺乏适用于移动皮肤镜的临床验证数据集。

Method: 方法包含三个组成部分：(1) 移动皮肤镜图像采集的标准操作规程(SOP)；(2) 包含16个结构化元数据字段、分为6个临床导向模块、采用ISIC兼容标注的信息模型；(3) 多阶段专家诊断标签验证（初始临床标注、三位专家共识审查、所有恶性病变的组织学确认）。

Result: 在2025年6月至2026年5月间收集了来自443名患者的1026张皮肤镜图像，排除18张重复图像后，涵盖9种疾病类别；39个恶性病变（18例黑色素瘤、15例基底细胞癌、6例鳞状细胞癌）均经组织学验证。患者年龄2-90岁（中位数38），女性279人（63%），男性164人（37%）。每张图像附带专家标注的皮肤镜结构和明确的验证阶段字段。

Conclusion: 该数据集可作为临床验证的试点资源，适用于独立模型评估、领域偏移分析、可解释性研究及后续扩展。

Original Abstract: This study presents a methodology for constructing a clinically verified dataset of dermatoscopic images for medical informatics research. The relevance of the work is driven by the fact that the performance of automated diagnostic support systems depends not only on the volume of images, but also on the reproducibility of the image acquisition procedure, the completeness of structured metadata, and the reliability of diagnostic labels. International collections were primarily created under conditions that differ substantially from routine Russian outpatient practice and mobile dermatoscopy. The proposed methodology integrates three interconnected components: (1) a standard operating procedure (SOP) for acquiring images via mobile dermatoscopy, (2) an information model comprising 16 structured metadata fields organized into six clinically oriented blocks in ISIC-compatible notation, and (3) a multi-stage expert verification of diagnostic labels (initial clinical annotation, consensus review by three specialists, and histological confirmation of all malignant neoplasms). Using this methodology, a dataset of 1,026 unique dermatoscopic images from 443 patients was collected between June 2025 and May 2026. From 1,044 initial records, 18 duplicates were excluded. The dataset includes nine nosological categories; all 39 malignant lesions (18 melanomas, 15 basal cell carcinomas, and 6 squamous cell carcinomas) were histologically verified. Patient age ranged from 2 to 90 years (median 38), with 279 females (63%) and 164 males (37%). Each image is accompanied by expert-annotated dermatoscopic structures and an explicit verification_stage field indicating the level of diagnostic confirmation. The resulting dataset serves as a pilot clinically verified resource suitable for independent model evaluation, domain shift analysis, interpretability studies, and further expansion.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究提出了一种用于构建临床验证的皮肤镜影像数据集的方法论，以服务于医学信息学研究。该工作的意义在于，自动化诊断支持系统的性能不仅取决于图像数量，还取决于图像采集过程的可重复性、结构化元数据的完整性以及诊断标签的可靠性。现有的国际数据集主要在与俄罗斯常规门诊实践和移动皮肤镜检查差异显著的环境下创建。所提出的方法整合了三个相互关联的组成部分：(1) 基于移动皮肤镜进行图像采集的标准操作规程(SOP)；(2) 一个信息模型，包含16个结构化元数据字段，组织成六个临床导向模块，采用ISIC兼容标注格式；(3) 多阶段专家验证诊断标签（初始临床标注、三位专家共识审查，以及所有恶性病变的组织学确认）。采用该方法，在2025年6月至2026年5月期间收集了来自443名患者的1026张独特的皮肤镜影像数据集。从最初的1044条记录中排除了18张重复图像。该数据集涵盖9种疾病类别；所有39个恶性病变（18例黑色素瘤、15例基底细胞癌和6例鳞状细胞癌）均经组织学验证。患者年龄范围为2至90岁（中位年龄38岁），其中女性279人（63%），男性164人（37%）。每张图像均附有专家标注的皮肤镜结构以及一个明确的验证阶段字段，用于指示诊断确认的级别。所生成的数据集是一个经过临床验证的试点资源，适用于独立模型评估、领域偏移分析、可解释性研究及进一步扩展。

</details>


### [93] [Discrepancy Minimization Improves Cross-Hospital Robustness in Digital Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25175)
*Ben Vardi,Dana Schonberger,Yuval Friedmann,Zohar Yakhini,Iris Barshack,Alexander Loebel,Ariel Shamir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过局部最大均值差异（LMMD）目标对病理学基础模型进行微调，以提升其在域适应和域泛化场景下的跨医院鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 病理学基础模型在不同医院之间的鲁棒性有限，在源医院训练的分类器迁移到目标医院时性能会下降，亟需提升其跨域泛化能力。

Method: 采用局部最大均值差异（LMMD）目标对病理学基础模型进行微调，适用于两种场景：域适应（有未标注的目标医院数据）和域泛化（完全无目标医院数据）。

Result: 在多个病理学基础模型和任务上，补丁级和切片级实验均显示出一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: LMMD微调策略能有效提升病理学基础模型在不同医院间的跨域鲁棒性，且适用于域适应和域泛化两种常见场景。

Original Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) have advanced rapidly in recent years and support training classifiers for a range of histopathology tasks. However, their robustness across hospitals remains limited: performance often degrades when training a classifier on data from one hospital and evaluating it on another target hospital. We address this challenge by fine-tuning PFMs with a local maximum mean discrepancy (LMMD) objective that applies to two settings: domain adaptation, where unlabeled target-hospital data is available, and domain generalization, where target-hospital data is unavailable at all. Experiments at both the patch- and slide-level show consistent improvements across multiple PFMs and tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 病理学基础模型（PFMs）近年来发展迅速，能够为多种组织病理学任务训练分类器。然而，它们在不同医院间的鲁棒性仍然有限：当在一个医院的数据上训练分类器并在另一个目标医院上进行评估时，性能往往下降。我们通过使用局部最大均值差异（LMMD）目标对PFMs进行微调来应对这一挑战，该方法适用于两种场景：域适应（可获得未标注的目标医院数据）和域泛化（目标医院数据完全不可用）。在补丁级和切片级上的实验表明，多个PFMs和任务均取得了一致的改进。

</details>


### [94] [Injecting Image Guidance into Text-Conditioned Diffusion Models at Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25191)
*Agata Żywot,Iason Skylitsis,Thijmen Nijdam,Zoe Tzifa-Kratira,Derck Prinzhorn,Konrad Szewczyk,Aritra Bhowmik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉概念融合（VCF）方法，首次实现无需重新训练即可在推理时对文本到图像扩散模型（如Stable Diffusion）进行图像和文本双重条件控制，通过轻量级对齐器将CLIP图像特征映射到文本嵌入空间，有效迁移参考图像的风格、构图和色调。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到图像扩散模型（如Stable Diffusion）无法在推理时注入视觉引导（如草图、风格），已有方法要么需要计算昂贵的微调，要么依赖可能破坏语义对齐的风格迁移技术。

Method: VCF包含三个组件：(1) 轻量级对齐器，使用InfoNCE损失和交叉注意力重建损失将图像token映射到文本嵌入流形；(2) 融合策略，同时保留文本和视觉语义；(3) 可选的提示噪声优化（PNO）模块，用于测试时细化。

Result: 实验表明VCF能成功迁移参考图像的风格、构图和色调等视觉属性，同时保持对文本提示的忠实性。量化结果显示了文本对齐（CLIP分数）与视觉对应（LPIPS）之间的权衡，VCF在参考保真度上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: VCF是首个无需特定概念训练即可在推理时实现图像和文本双重条件控制的文本到图像扩散方法，有效兼顾了文本对齐与视觉特征迁移。

Original Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models like Stable Diffusion generate high-quality images from text, but lack a way to inject visual guidance (e.g. sketches, styles) at inference without retraining. Existing methods either require computationally expensive fine-tuning or rely on style transfer techniques that risk semantic misalignment with textual prompts. We introduce Visual Concept Fusion (VCF), the first method offering dual conditioning on both an image and text prompt at inference time without any concept-specific training. VCF enables visual concept injection into Stable Diffusion by aligning CLIP image features with the text embedding space. VCF consists of three components: (1) a lightweight aligner that maps image tokens to the text embedding manifold using InfoNCE and cross-attention reconstruction losses, (2) a fusion strategy that preserves both textual and visual semantics, and (3) an optional Prompt-Noise Optimization (PNO) module for test-time refinement. Our experiments demonstrate that VCF successfully transfers visual attributes including style, composition, and color palette from reference images while maintaining prompt adherence. Quantitative results show a trade-off between text alignment (CLIP score) and visual correspondence (LPIPS), with VCF outperforming baselines in reference fidelity.

中文摘要翻译: 像Stable Diffusion这样的文本到图像扩散模型能从文本生成高质量图像，但缺乏在推理时无需重新训练即可注入视觉引导（如草图、风格）的方法。现有方法要么需要计算昂贵的微调，要么依赖可能破坏文本提示语义对齐的风格迁移技术。我们提出视觉概念融合（VCF），这是首个无需任何特定概念训练即可在推理时对图像和文本提示进行双重条件控制的方法。VCF通过将CLIP图像特征与文本嵌入空间对齐，实现了向Stable Diffusion注入视觉概念。VCF包含三个组件：(1) 轻量级对齐器，使用InfoNCE损失和交叉注意力重建损失将图像token映射到文本嵌入流形；(2) 融合策略，同时保留文本和视觉语义；(3) 可选的提示噪声优化（PNO）模块，用于测试时细化。我们的实验表明，VCF能够成功迁移参考图像的视觉属性，包括风格、构图和色调，同时保持对提示的忠实性。量化结果显示在文本对齐（CLIP分数）和视觉对应（LPIPS）之间存在权衡，VCF在参考保真度上优于基线方法。

</details>


### [95] [SpongeBob: Sync-Aware Harmonious Audio-Visual Generative Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25193)
*Sen Liang,Cong Wang,Fengbin Guan,Zhentao Yu,Yiting Lu,Yuanzhi Wang,Yuan Zhou,Xin Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpongeBob，首个端到端音视频联合编辑框架，通过双向跨模态交互解决音视频不同步和上下文冲突问题，在Sync-C和Ctx-F1指标上分别提升30%和12.5%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频编辑方法采用解耦流水线，缺乏双向模态交互，导致（i）音视频不同步和（ii）生成音频与保留内容之间的上下文冲突。

Method: 提出SpongeBob框架，包含同步感知机制（通过双向注意力、时间对齐和空间约束实现视觉编辑与声音事件对齐）和上下文感知模块（利用声学和视觉上下文注意力防止语义冲突），并引入同步保持训练与引导（SPTG）增强对齐而不降低质量。

Result: 在Sync-C指标上提升30%，在Ctx-F1指标上提升12.5%，显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: SpongeBob作为首个端到端音视频联合编辑框架，通过双向跨模态交互有效解决了音视频不同步和上下文冲突问题，为音视频联合编辑提供了新的范式。

Original Abstract: Visual and acoustic events in the physical world are inherently coupled, yet existing video editing methods typically adopt decoupled pipelines, lacking bidirectional modality interaction. This results in two key limitations: (i) audio-visual desynchronization and (ii) contextual conflicts between generated audio and preserved content. To address these, we propose SpongeBob, the first end-to-end audio-visual joint editing framework featuring bidirectional cross-modal interaction. For synchronization, a Sync-Aware Mechanism aligns visual edits with sound events via bidirectional attention, temporal alignment, and spatial constraints. For contextual consistency, a Context-Aware Module leverages acoustic and visual context attention to prevent semantic clashes. Additionally, we introduce Sync-Preserving Training and Guidance (SPTG) to enhance alignment without degrading quality. Due to the scarcity of paired data, we construct a scalable data pipeline and a large-scale subject-level dataset. We also propose SpongeBob-Bench for systematic evaluation. Experiments show SpongeBob significantly outperforms existing baselines, improving Sync-C by 30% and Ctx-F1 by 12.5%. Our project page is available at: https://hy-spongebob.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 物理世界中的视觉和听觉事件本质上是耦合的，然而现有的视频编辑方法通常采用解耦的流水线，缺乏双向模态交互。这导致了两个关键限制：（i）音视频不同步以及（ii）生成音频与保留内容之间的上下文冲突。为解决这些问题，我们提出了SpongeBob——首个具有双向跨模态交互的端到端音视频联合编辑框架。针对同步问题，同步感知机制通过双向注意力、时间对齐和空间约束将视觉编辑与声音事件对齐。针对上下文一致性问题，上下文感知模块利用声学和视觉上下文注意力防止语义冲突。此外，我们引入了同步保持训练与引导（SPTG），以在不降低质量的情况下增强对齐。由于配对数据的稀缺性，我们构建了一个可扩展的数据流水线和一个大规模的主体级数据集。我们还提出了SpongeBob-Bench以进行系统评估。实验表明，SpongeBob显著优于现有基线，Sync-C提升了30%，Ctx-F1提升了12.5%。我们的项目页面位于：https://hy-spongebob.github.io/。

</details>


### [96] [Baton: Explicit Semantic Blueprints for Joint Video-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25195)
*Shuyuan Tu,Qi Tian,Zihan Yang,Yue Wu,Xintong Han,Weijie Kong,Jiangfeng Xiong,Jian-Wei Zhang,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Baton框架，通过显式语义规划（VA-Planner生成双模态对齐的规划token + Relative Semantic RoPE位置编码）解决开源扩散模型在音视频联合生成中因缺乏共享长期规划导致的跨模态对齐脆弱问题，在多个基准上取得定性定量最优结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开源扩散模型依赖粗粒度文本嵌入引导音视频去噪，丢弃了细粒度语义且缺乏共享的长期规划，导致去噪轨迹不协调、跨模态对齐脆弱，无法生成稳定同步的音视频内容，尤其难以处理复杂语义推理场景。

Method: 提出Baton框架，核心包括：(1) VA-Planner：多模态语言模型配备双语义对齐塔，通过可学习查询对视频和音频特征进行交叉注意力，生成语义对齐的视频和音频规划token作为关键帧级蓝图；(2) 将规划token通过交叉注意力层注入扩散骨干，提供与粗文本嵌入互补的时序引导；(3) Relative Semantic RoPE：相对位置编码将规划token和扩散潜变量映射到共享时空坐标框架，实现位置对应的语义注意力。

Result: 在多个基准上，Baton在定性和定量评估中均取得了优于现有方法的生成效果，验证了其有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 通过显式语义规划建立共享蓝图来协调音视频去噪轨迹，可显著提升跨模态对齐质量和生成内容的稳定性与同步性，为联合音视频生成提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Current open-source diffusion models struggle to generate stable and synchronized audio-visual content, particularly in scenarios demanding complex semantic reasoning. The root cause is that existing methods rely on coarse text embeddings from off-the-shelf encoders to guide audio-video denoising, which discards fine-grained semantics and, critically, lacks a shared long-horizon plan, leading to uncoordinated denoising trajectories and fragile cross-modal alignment. We propose Baton, the first framework that introduces explicit semantic planning into joint video-audio generation. Our key insight is that complementing coarse text guidance with semantically rich, modality-aware planned tokens, jointly reasoned and mutually aligned before denoising, can simultaneously restore fine-grained semantic detail and establish a shared blueprint that coordinates both audio and video denoising trajectories. Concretely, Baton first introduces the VA-Planner, a multimodal language model equipped with dual semantic alignment towers, where learnable queries cross-attend to both video and audio features to produce a pair of semantically aligned video and audio planned tokens as keyframe-level blueprints. These planned tokens are injected into the diffusion backbone via cross-attention layers, providing temporally grounded guidance complementary to coarse text embeddings. Since planned tokens do not share one-to-one spatial-temporal correspondence with diffusion latents, we further propose Relative Semantic RoPE, a relative positional encoding that maps planned tokens and latents into a shared spatial-temporal coordinate frame, enabling each latent to accurately attend to its positionally corresponding semantic cues. Experiments on benchmarks show the effectiveness of Baton both qualitatively and quantitatively.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的开源扩散模型难以生成稳定且同步的音视频内容，尤其是在需要复杂语义推理的场景中。根本原因在于现有方法依赖现成编码器生成的粗粒度文本嵌入来引导音频-视频去噪，这不仅丢弃了细粒度语义，更关键的是缺乏共享的长期规划，导致去噪轨迹不协调且跨模态对齐脆弱。本文提出Baton，这是首个将显式语义规划引入联合视频-音频生成的框架。我们的核心洞察是：用语义丰富、模态感知的规划token来补充粗粒度文本引导，这些token在去噪前经过联合推理和相互对齐，能够同时恢复细粒度语义细节并建立协调音视频去噪轨迹的共享蓝图。具体来说，Baton首先引入VA-Planner，一个配备双语义对齐塔的多模态语言模型，其中可学习的查询同时交叉注意力到视频和音频特征上，生成一对语义对齐的视频和音频规划token作为关键帧级蓝图。这些规划token通过交叉注意力层注入扩散骨干，提供与粗粒度文本嵌入互补的时序引导。由于规划token与扩散潜变量不具有一一对应的时空对应关系，我们进一步提出相对语义RoPE，一种相对位置编码，将规划token和潜变量映射到共享的时空坐标框架中，使每个潜变量能够准确关注其位置对应的语义线索。基准实验在定性和定量两方面均证明了Baton的有效性。

</details>


### [97] [Multi-view Consistent 3D Gaussian Head Avatars 'without' Multi-view Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25220)
*Aviral Chharia,Fernando De la Torre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MVCHead提出了一种无需多视角数据或3D监督，仅从随机采样的2D图像中学习高质量3D高斯头模的方法，通过层级状态空间模型和SE(3)多视图判分器实现多视角一致性，并发布了首个大规模3D高斯头模数据集FaceGS-10K。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D头部头像生成方法依赖多视角数据集、3D捕获或中间2D视图合成，这些数据获取成本高、条件苛刻。本文旨在仅从随机采样的2D图像中学习3D头部模型，无需多视角数据、3D监督或中间视图生成，从而降低数据需求并提升方法实用性。

Method: 提出MVCHead模型，核心包括：(1) Hierarchical State Space (HiSS)模块，从粗到细逐步细化3D高斯并捕获长程依赖；(2) Hierarchical Bi-directional State Scan (HiBiSS)，修改Mamba的单向扫描为双向层级扫描，对齐多视角不一致性最强的轴；(3) SE(3)多视图判分器，评判自渲染视图是否来自同一3D配置，无需真实多视角对即可奖励跨视图像素对齐。

Result: MVCHead在感知质量上达到最先进水平，在纹理和几何一致性上超越先前方法，形状一致性相当。同时发布了FaceGS-10K数据集，这是首个大规模可直接使用的3D高斯头部资产数据集。

Conclusion: MVCHead证明了仅从随机2D图像学习具有多视角一致性的3D高斯头模是可行的，为降低3D头部生成的数据门槛提供了新范式，FaceGS-10K数据集也为该领域研究提供了标准化基准。

Original Abstract: High-fidelity 3D Gaussian head avatar generation is critical for applications such as AR/VR, telepresence, and digital humans. Existing methods depend on multi-view datasets, 3D captures, or intermediate 2D view synthesis. In contrast, we learn both conditional and unconditional 3D head models from randomly sampled 2D images alone, without using multi-view data, 3D supervision, or intermediate view generation. We introduce MVCHead, a single-shot state space model that enforces multi-view consistency (MVC) directly in the 3D representation while regressing 3D Gaussians under these constraints. At its core, we propose a Hierarchical State Space (HiSS) block that progressively refines Gaussians from coarse to fine, while capturing long-range dependencies. Within each HiSS block, we modify Mamba's standard unidirectional scan with the proposed Hierarchical Bi-directional State Scan (HiBiSS) that aligns recurrence with the axes along which multi-view inconsistencies are strongest. Finally, we design an SE(3) Multi-view Critic that judges whether a set of self-renders arises from a single underlying 3D configuration, rewarding cross-view pixel alignment without observing real multi-view pairs. MVCHead achieves state-of-the-art perceptual quality, surpasses prior methods in both texture and geometric consistency, and maintains comparable shape consistency. To demonstrate scalability, we release FaceGS-10K, the first large-scale dataset of ready-to-use 3D Gaussian head assets for training and evaluation of 3D head models. Project Page and code: https://humansensinglab.github.io/MVCHead/

中文摘要翻译: 高保真度的3D高斯头部头像生成对于AR/VR、远程呈现和数字人等重要应用至关重要。现有方法依赖于多视角数据集、3D捕获或中间2D视图合成。相比之下，我们仅从随机采样的2D图像中学习条件性和无条件性的3D头部模型，无需使用多视角数据、3D监督或中间视图生成。我们提出MVCHead，这是一个单次前馈的状态空间模型，它直接在3D表示中强制实现多视角一致性（MVC），同时在这些约束下回归3D高斯参数。其核心是我们提出的层级状态空间（HiSS）模块，该模块从粗到细逐步细化高斯参数，同时捕获长程依赖关系。在每个HiSS模块内部，我们将Mamba的标准单向扫描改进为所提出的层级双向状态扫描（HiBiSS），该扫描将递归方向与多视角不一致性最强的轴对齐。最后，我们设计了一个SE(3)多视图判分器，用于评判一组自渲染视图是否源自单一的底层3D配置，从而在无需观察到真实多视角对的情况下奖励跨视图像素对齐。MVCHead在感知质量上达到了最先进水平，在纹理和几何一致性方面超越了先前方法，并保持了可比的形状一致性。为了展示可扩展性，我们发布了FaceGS-10K——首个用于训练和评估3D头部模型的大规模可直接使用的3D高斯头部资产数据集。项目页面和代码：https://humansensinglab.github.io/MVCHead/

</details>


### [98] [Semantics-Guided Multimodal Masked Autoencoder Pretraining for 3D BEV Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25262)
*Prabuddhi Wariyapperuma,Rajitha de Silva,Marc Hanheide,Thomas Bohné,Leonardo Guevara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种语义引导的多模态掩码自编码器框架，通过在预训练中引入语义信息（语义引导的LiDAR体素掩码+辅助点级语义解码分支），提升了3D BEV目标检测的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法对相机和LiDAR输入采用均匀随机掩码，平等对待所有区域，且仅通过掩码重建学习表示，忽视了不同区域的语义重要性差异。

Method: 提出语义引导的多模态掩码自编码器框架，包含两个组件：(1) 语义引导的LiDAR体素掩码，保留语义重要的LiDAR区域；(2) 辅助的点级LiDAR语义解码分支，在重建之外注入语义引导。

Result: 在BEVFusion 3D目标检测上，相比UniM2AE基线：语义引导的LiDAR体素掩码在nuScenes mini验证集上提升+1.49% mAP和+1.66% NDS；解码器侧点语义监督提升+1.39% mAP和+3.22% NDS。

Conclusion: 语义引导的多模态掩码自编码器预训练策略能有效提升3D BEV目标检测性能，证实了在预训练阶段引入语义信息的有效性。

Original Abstract: Accurate 3D bird's-eye view (BEV) object detection is essential for autonomous driving, and depends strongly on effective multimodal representations from complementary sensors such as cameras and LiDAR. Multimodal masked autoencoders have shown strong potential for learning such representations for downstream 3D BEV object detection. However, existing methods typically apply uniform random masking to camera and LiDAR inputs, treating all regions equally, and learn representations only through masked reconstruction. We propose a semantics-guided multimodal masked autoencoder framework that introduces semantic information during pretraining through two separate components: (i) semantics-guided LiDAR voxel masking, which preserves semantically important LiDAR regions more strongly, and (ii) an auxiliary point-wise LiDAR semantic decoder branch that injects semantic guidance in addition to reconstruction. On BEVFusion 3D object detection, our semantics-guided pretraining strategy improves performance on the nuScenes mini validation set compared to the standard UniM2AE baseline: semantics-guided LiDAR voxel masking yields +1.49% mean Average Precision (mAP) and +1.66% nuScenes Detection Score (NDS), while decoder-side point semantic supervision yields +1.39% mAP and +3.22% NDS over the baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的3D鸟瞰图（BEV）目标检测对于自动驾驶至关重要，并且高度依赖于来自互补传感器（如相机和LiDAR）的有效多模态表示。多模态掩码自编码器在学习用于下游3D BEV目标检测的此类表示方面表现出巨大潜力。然而，现有方法通常对相机和LiDAR输入采用均匀随机掩码，平等对待所有区域，并且仅通过掩码重建学习表示。我们提出了一种语义引导的多模态掩码自编码器框架，该框架通过两个独立组件在预训练期间引入语义信息：（i）语义引导的LiDAR体素掩码，它更强烈地保留语义重要的LiDAR区域，以及（ii）辅助的点级LiDAR语义解码分支，它在重建之外注入语义引导。在BEVFusion 3D目标检测上，与标准UniM2AE基线相比，我们的语义引导预训练策略在nuScenes mini验证集上提升了性能：语义引导的LiDAR体素掩码带来了+1.49%的平均精度（mAP）和+1.66%的nuScenes检测分数（NDS），而解码器侧点语义监督相比基线带来了+1.39%的mAP和+3.22%的NDS提升。

</details>


### [99] [DeltaCam: Differential Intrinsic Camera Modeling for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25266)
*Debabrata Mandal,Zhihan Peng,Yujie Wang,Praneeth Chakravarthula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeltaCam提出了一种基于Δ参数化的神经摄像机适配器框架，通过建模摄像机内参的相对变化而非绝对状态，实现了对景深、曝光、镜头畸变等摄影行为的可控视频生成，并支持从合成数据到真实场景的迁移。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成模型主要关注摄像机外参（如姿态、运动）控制，而忽略了内参（如焦距、光圈、ISO等）的影响；同时缺乏大规模带有精确时变摄像机元数据的真实视频数据集，导致模型难以学习绝对摄像机参数化。因此需要一种不依赖绝对标签、能控制摄像机内参行为的可控视频生成方法。

Method: 提出DeltaCam框架，核心是Δ参数化的神经摄像机适配器，学习摄像机运动和内参的**相对变化**而非绝对状态。通过合成视频数据进行差分公式训练，降低对真实标签的依赖；通过两种机制扩展到真实场景：1）在真实图像-元数据对上微调控制；2）提取解耦嵌入用于隐式视频到视频的风格迁移。

Result: 实验表明DeltaCam能够实现平滑一致的摄像机内参控制（焦距、光圈、ISO、色温、镜头畸变等），支持摄像机一致的视频生成和编辑操作，在真实场景摄影模拟中取得了优于现有模型的效果。

Conclusion: 通过在场景内容与内在成像行为之间进行有效解耦，DeltaCam建立了一种实用且可扩展的方法，连接了合成控制与真实摄影模拟，为视频生成模型中的摄像机内参控制提供了可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Incorporating camera intrinsics into video generation models offers a principled way to control not only scene dynamics but also the imaging process that governs visual appearance. Prior work has primarily focused on extrinsic control, such as camera pose and motion, while treating intrinsic camera parameters as implicit or fixed. A key bottleneck is the lack of large-scale video datasets with accurate and diverse temporally varying camera metadata, which makes learning absolute camera parameterizations difficult. As a result, current models struggle to incorporate photographic camera behavior, including depth-of-field transitions, exposure variations, lens distortions, and color processing, in a controllable and temporally consistent manner. We introduce DeltaCam, a video diffusion framework that models camera behavior through $Δ$-parameterized neural camera adaptors, operating on relative changes in camera motion and intrinsics instead of absolute states. By learning this differential formulation from synthetic video data, we mitigate reliance on precise real-world camera labels and enable smooth, consistent control over imaging factors such as focal length, aperture, ISO, color temperature, and lens distortion. We extend this framework to real-world footage through two mechanisms: finetuning the controls on real image-metadata pairs for precise shot matching, and extracting disentangled embeddings for implicit video-to-video style transfer without requiring explicit camera parameters. By effectively separating scene content from intrinsic imaging behavior, DeltaCam enables camera-consistent video generation and editing operations that are difficult to achieve with existing models. Ultimately, our results establish a practical and scalable approach for bridging synthetic control and real-world photographic emulation.

中文摘要翻译: 将摄像机内参融入视频生成模型，为控制场景动态以及支配视觉外观的成像过程提供了一种有原则的方法。先前工作主要关注外参控制（如摄像机姿态和运动），而将摄像机内参视为隐含或固定的。关键瓶颈在于缺乏具有精确且多样化的时变摄像机元数据的大规模视频数据集，这使得学习绝对摄像机参数化变得困难。因此，当前模型难以以可控且时间一致的方式融入摄影级摄像机行为，包括景深转换、曝光变化、镜头畸变和色彩处理。我们提出了DeltaCam，一个通过Δ参数化神经摄像机适配器来建模摄像机行为的视频扩散框架，该框架基于摄像机运动和内参的相对变化而非绝对状态进行操作。通过从合成视频数据中学习这种差分公式，我们降低了对精确真实世界摄像机标签的依赖，并实现了对焦距、光圈、ISO、色温和镜头畸变成像因素的平滑一致控制。我们通过两种机制将该框架扩展到真实世界 footage：在真实图像-元数据对上微调控制以实现精确的镜头匹配，以及提取解耦嵌入用于隐式视频到视频风格迁移，无需显式摄像机参数。通过有效分离场景内容与内在成像行为，DeltaCam能够实现现有模型难以达成的摄像机一致的视频生成和编辑操作。最终，我们的结果建立了一种实用且可扩展的方法，用于连接合成控制与真实世界摄影模拟。

</details>


### [100] [Geometry-Aware Image Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25294)
*Junho Lee,Kwanseok Kim,Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现自然图像的语义信息主要编码在方向分量中，而模长分量可由全局均值近似，提出基于流匹配的球面生成模型（SOT-CFM和SFM），在自然图像生成上优于欧几里得基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成模型在流形约束场景中展现了几何感知建模的能力，但在自然图像领域仍局限于欧几里得假设，未能利用数据内在的几何结构。

Method: 提出两种方法：Spherical Optimal Transport Flow Matching (SOT-CFM)，利用角度距离进行最优传输流匹配；Spherical Flow Matching (SFM)，直接在球流形上约束动力学过程。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的几何感知方法在自然图像生成任务上优于欧几里得基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文在黎曼流形建模与自然图像生成之间架起了桥梁，提供了新的视角。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in generative models highlight the power of geometry-aware modeling in manifold-constrained settings. Yet, for natural images, the field remains confined to Euclidean assumptions, failing to exploit the potential of intrinsic geometric structures within the data. In this work, we investigate the geometry of natural images and observe that semantic information is predominantly encoded in directional components, while norm components can be approximated by the global average. This property holds across both RGB and latent spaces, suggesting that natural images can be effectively modeled on a hypersphere. Building on this finding, we introduce Spherical Optimal Transport Flow Matching (SOT-CFM), which utilizes angular distance, and Spherical Flow Matching (SFM), which constrains dynamics directly on the manifold. Our experiments demonstrate that these geometry-aware methods achieve superior performance against Euclidean baselines. Ultimately, this work provides a novel perspective that bridges the gap between Riemannian manifold-based modeling and natural image generation.

中文摘要翻译: 生成模型的最新进展突显了在流形约束场景中几何感知建模的强大能力。然而，在自然图像领域，该领域仍然局限于欧几里得假设，未能利用数据内在的几何结构。本文研究了自然图像的几何特性，观察到语义信息主要编码在方向分量中，而模长分量可通过全局均值近似。这一性质在RGB空间和潜在空间中均成立，表明自然图像可以在超球面上进行有效建模。基于这一发现，我们引入了球面最优传输流匹配（SOT-CFM），该方法利用角度距离，以及球面流匹配（SFM），该方法直接在流形上约束动力学过程。实验表明，这些几何感知方法在性能上优于欧几里得基线。最终，本文提供了一个新的视角，弥合了基于黎曼流形的建模与自然图像生成之间的差距。

</details>


### [101] [Recursive Class Connectivity Classification (R3C) Applied to Binary Image Segmentation for Improved Infant Fingerprint Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25307)
*Joao Leonardo Harres Dall Agnol,Luiz Fernando Puttow Southier,Jefferson Tales 0liva,Marcelo Teixeira,Rodrigo Mineto,Marcelo Filipa,Dalcimar Casanova,Erick Oliveira Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R3C框架，通过迭代反馈机制改进现有增强方法的二值分割输出，在不需训练数据的情况下提升婴儿指纹识别准确率，使儿童TAR提升高达4%，新生儿提升超40%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有指纹增强方法主要针对成人设计，不支持高分辨率扫描仪获取的婴儿指纹图像，导致儿童指纹识别率远低于成人。

Method: R3C（递归类连通性分类）框架通过反复将分类后的图像反馈回分类过程，并将每次中间分割结果与原始输入图像结合，迭代细化现有增强方法的二值分割输出，延伸脊线结构，无需修改底层分类器，无需训练数据。

Result: 在三个指纹数据集上使用四种不同增强分类器的实验表明，R3C使儿童TAR提升高达4%，新生儿提升超40%；定性分析显示R3C能重新连接断裂的脊线模式，改善分割的视觉质量。

Conclusion: R3C独立于所使用的增强方法，为改进二值分割提供了一种灵活且广泛适用的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Image enhancement plays a crucial role in infant fingerprint matching, as child-specific characteristics such as smaller finger dimensions and thinner ridge structures often degrade image quality during acquisition. To address these limitations, enrollment typically depends on specialized highresolution scanners, which most existing enhancement methods are not designed to support. Consequently, identification rates for children remain significantly lower than those achieved with adult fingerprints. This study introduces Recursive Class Connectivity Classification (R3C), a novel framework that iteratively refines binary segmentation outputs from existing enhancement methods by extending ridge structures. R3C does not require modifications to the underlying classifier and operates without training data, which is not currently available for infant fingerprints. Instead, the method improves segmentation by repeatedly feeding the classified image back into the classification process, while combining each intermediate segmentation with the original input image. Experiments conducted on three fingerprint datasets using four different enhancement classifiers show that R3C can increase the True Acceptance Rate (TAR) by up to 4% for children and over 40% for newborns, compared to using the enhancement methods alone. A qualitative analysis further demonstrates that R3C reconnects fragmented ridge patterns, improving the visual quality of segmentation. Because it functions independently of the enhancement method used, R3C provides a flexible and broadly applicable solution for improving binary segmentation.

中文摘要翻译: 图像增强在婴儿指纹匹配中扮演着关键角色，因为儿童特有的特征（如较小的手指尺寸和更薄的脊线结构）通常会在采集过程中降低图像质量。为解决这些限制，注册过程通常依赖于专门的高分辨率扫描仪，而现有的大多数增强方法并不支持这种设备。因此，儿童的识别率仍然显著低于成人指纹。本研究引入了递归类连通性分类（R3C），这是一种新颖的框架，通过延伸脊线结构来迭代细化现有增强方法的二值分割输出。R3C无需修改底层分类器，并且无需训练数据（目前婴儿指纹尚无可用的训练数据）。该方法通过将分类后的图像反复反馈回分类过程，同时将每次中间分割结果与原始输入图像相结合，从而改进分割效果。在三个指纹数据集上使用四种不同增强分类器进行的实验表明，与单独使用增强方法相比，R3C可将儿童的真实接受率（TAR）提升高达4%，新生儿的TAR提升超过40%。定性分析进一步表明，R3C能够重新连接断裂的脊线模式，从而改善分割的视觉质量。由于R3C独立于所使用的增强方法，因此它为改进二值分割提供了一种灵活且广泛适用的解决方案。

</details>


### [102] [Perceive-then-Plan: Layout-as-Policy for Monocular 3D Scene Layout Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25326)
*Junwei Zhou,Yu-Wing Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Layout-as-Policy (LaP)方法，将单目3D布局估计转化为感知-规划问题，利用视觉-语言模型进行迭代优化，生成在物理合理性和视觉一致性上更优的3D场景布局。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从单张图像构建结构化3D场景布局需要协调视觉观测与物理空间约束，直接预测难以有效处理全局约束和复杂物体交互，现有方法缺乏迭代修正机制。

Method: 提出Layout-as-Policy (LaP)：采用感知-规划两阶段框架。Perceiver模块进行几何增强的3D物体定位；Planner模块将布局优化视为策略学习问题，通过离散动作（平移、旋转、缩放）迭代细化场景假设。训练结合监督轨迹初始化和基于偏好的优化，无需显式奖励工程。

Result: 实验表明该方法生成的布局在物理连贯性和视觉观测对齐度上更优，并能自然支持场景编辑与操作等下游任务。

Conclusion: 将布局估计从一次性预测转化为迭代优化过程，能更好地处理全局约束和复杂物体交互，生成更物理合理且与视觉观测一致的3D场景布局。

Original Abstract: Building structured 3D scene layouts from a single image requires reconciling visual observations with physical and spatial constraints, a challenge that is difficult to address with direct prediction alone. In this work, we formulate monocular 3D layout estimation as a perceive-then-plan problem with vision-language models, where a Perceiver first grounds the 3D objects and then a Planner iteratively refines the scene hypothesis through actions that improve physical plausibility while preserving consistency with the input image. We propose Layout-as-Policy (LaP), which casts the planning stage as a policy learning problem: 3D layouts are represented as structured states, and refined via discrete actions such as translation, rotation, and rescaling. Starting from an observation-aligned initialization with the geometry-enhanced Perceiver, the LaP Planner is trained to produce action sequences that progressively resolve geometric inconsistencies and enforce realistic spatial relations. To enable effective learning, we combine supervised trajectory initialization with preference-based optimization, allowing the model to learn corrective behaviors without requiring explicit reward engineering. This formulation transforms layout estimation from a one-shot prediction task into an iterative refinement process, enabling better handling of global constraints and complex object interactions. Experiments demonstrate that our approach produces layouts that are more physically coherent and better aligned with visual observations, while naturally supporting downstream tasks such as scene editing and manipulation.

中文摘要翻译: 从单张图像构建结构化3D场景布局需要协调视觉观测与物理和空间约束，这一挑战难以仅通过直接预测来解决。本文将单目3D布局估计建模为视觉-语言模型下的感知-规划问题：感知器首先定位3D物体，然后规划器通过迭代优化场景假设，在保持与输入图像一致性的同时提升物理合理性。我们提出Layout-as-Policy (LaP)，将规划阶段转化为策略学习问题：3D布局被表示为结构化状态，通过平移、旋转和缩放等离散动作进行细化。从几何增强感知器生成的观测对齐初始化出发，LaP规划器被训练生成逐步解决几何不一致性并强制执行真实空间关系的动作序列。为实现有效学习，我们将监督轨迹初始化与基于偏好的优化相结合，使模型无需显式奖励工程设计即可学习修正行为。该公式将布局估计从一次性预测任务转变为迭代优化过程，能够更好地处理全局约束和复杂物体交互。实验表明，我们的方法生成的布局在物理连贯性上更强，与视觉观测对齐更好，并能自然支持场景编辑与操作等下游任务。

</details>


### [103] [Teaching Video Generators to Remember: Eliciting Dynamic Memory for Out-of-Sight State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25333)
*Tianshuo Xu,Yichen Xie,Depu Meng,Chensheng Peng,Quentin Herau,Bo Jiang,Yihan Hu,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReMind通过面向记忆的数据构造、事件感知训练和缓存适配，让视频扩散Transformer在帧被中断时仍能从KV缓存中检索历史状态，实现动态记忆行为，在STEVO-Bench和图像到视频生成任务上取得最优结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在观测中断时会冻结隐藏状态，无法维持动态演化。虽然预训练视频扩散Transformer的KV缓存机制已具备非局部检索能力，但很少被训练用作动态记忆。

Method: 1) 构建100+动态事件分类法，制作含VLM过滤真实视频、生成硬动态、合成相机循环和记忆中断增强的相机标注训练集；2) 将每个片段转换为带保护锚点、退化区间和显式时间间隔的帧图；3) 设计节点结构课程（节点丢弃、噪声记忆、前沿延续、参考缓存训练）迫使模型跨中断检索；4) 提出PM-RoPE，以单注意力代价实现空时检索并保留预训练通路。

Result: 在STEVO-Bench和恢复任务上取得最佳综合得分；图像到视频评估表明该课程不会导致灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: ReMind成功将视频扩散Transformer的预训练KV缓存转化为动态记忆，在不遗忘已有能力的前提下显著提升了模型对观测中断的处理能力。

Original Abstract: Video world models should maintain evolving states when evidence is unobserved, yet current generators often freeze hidden states upon interruption. This is not simply a capacity problem: pretrained video diffusion transformers already possess KV-cache mechanisms capable of non-local retrieval, but they are rarely trained to use them as dynamic memory. We introduce ReMind, a framework eliciting dynamic memory behavior via memory-oriented data, event-aware training, and cache adaptation. Organized around a taxonomy of 100+ dynamic events, we build a camera-annotated training mixture combining VLM-filtered real videos, generated hard dynamics, synthetic camera loops, and memory-interruption augmentations. Each clip is converted into a frame graph with protected anchors, degraded intervals, and explicit temporal gaps. A node-structured curriculum, including node-drop, noisy memory, frontier continuation, and reference-cache training, forces the model to retrieve relevant past states across interruptions rather than relying solely on local continuity. PM-RoPE, an elegant camera-phase RoPE extension, unlocks spatiotemporal retrieval at a single-attention cost while preserving pretrained pathways. ReMind achieves the best overall scores on STEVO-Bench and recovery tasks. Furthermore, general image-to-video evaluations confirm this curriculum avoids catastrophic forgetting. We will open-source our code, data, and models.

中文摘要翻译: 视频世界模型应在证据未观测到时维持状态的演化，然而当前的生成器在中断时通常会冻结隐藏状态。这并非单纯的能力问题：预训练的视频扩散Transformer已经具备能够进行非局部检索的KV缓存机制，但它们很少被训练用作动态记忆。我们提出ReMind，一个通过面向记忆的数据构造、事件感知训练和缓存适配来激发动态记忆行为的框架。围绕100+动态事件的分类法，我们构建了一个相机标注的训练集混合物，包含VLM过滤的真实视频、生成的硬动态、合成相机循环和记忆中断增强。每个片段被转换为带有保护锚点、退化区间和显式时间间隔的帧图。节点结构化的课程——包括节点丢弃、噪声记忆、前沿延续和参考缓存训练——迫使模型在中断发生时检索相关历史状态，而非仅仅依赖局部连续性。我们提出PM-RoPE，一种优雅的相机相位RoPE扩展，以单注意力代价实现时空检索，同时保留预训练通路。ReMind在STEVO-Bench和恢复任务上取得了最佳综合得分。此外，通用图像到视频评估证实了这一课程设计不会导致灾难性遗忘。我们将开源我们的代码、数据和模型。

</details>


### [104] [Toward Native Multimodal Modeling: A Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25343)
*Siyu An,Junru Lu,Junnan Dong,Qiufeng Wang,Yinghui Li,Weizhi Fei,Zichao Yu,Zheng Yuan,Biao Liu,Haopeng Wang,Renzhao Liang,Yixuan Yang,Yunhang Shen,Bo Ke,Keyu Chen,Linhao Luo,Difan Zou,Xiao Huang,Di Yin,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究从传统多模态建模（后期融合）向原生多模态建模（NMM）的范式转变，定义了架构原生性（中期融合与早期融合），基于输入-输出对偶性将现有原生模型分为三类（多到文本、多到目标、多到多），并从工业视角全面阐述了原生建模的端到端流水线，包括架构协调、数据策展、训练配方、推理部署和评估体系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态建模主要依赖后期融合（将编码器与冻结的语言骨干拼装），缺乏模态间的内在整合，而原生多模态建模（NMM）的设计空间尚未被充分定义和形式化，亟需一个系统化的路线图来指导这一范式转变。

Method: (1) 明确定义架构原生性，区分中期融合和早期融合与非原生范式；(2) 基于输入-输出对偶性将现有原生模型分为三类：多到文本（跨模态理解）、多到目标（场景导向生成）、多到多（对称输入-输出的统一建模）；(3) 从工业视角系统拆解端到端流水线，涵盖架构协调、大规模数据策展、全栈训练配方、推理部署及全面评估。

Result: 提出了一个形式化的原生多模态建模路线图和分类体系，明确了从后期融合向原生范式转变的关键设计维度，并提供了完整的工业级实践指南。

Conclusion: 理解和生成无缝共存的统一Transformer范式是原生多模态建模的最终框架，本文为社区提供了从非原生到原生建模转变的系统化路线图和工业级实践方案。

Original Abstract: Multimodal modeling represents a vital step from modality-agnostic reasoning toward world modeling. While early approaches predominantly rely on late-fusion that assembles encoders and frozen language backbones with output heads, recent efforts have shifted the paradigm toward native multimodal modeling (NMM) with the intrinsic integration of modalities for superior multimodal performance. Despite its potential, the design space of native architectures remains insufficiently defined. In this paper, we present the community with a formalized roadmap for this transition. Specifically, we formally define the architectural nativity, distinguishing mid-fusion and early-fusion from non-native paradigms. We further organize the existing native models through the lens of input-output duality into three categories: (i) Multi-to-Text for cross-modal comprehension with text-only output; (ii) Multi-to-Target for scenario-oriented generation, e.g., image, audio and video generation, and (iii) Multi-to-Multi for unified modeling with symmetric input-output. We deliver a comprehensive and industrial-grade investigation into the transition toward the definitive NMM framework, where understanding and generation seamlessly coexist within a unified transformer paradigm. We systematically unpack the end-to-end pipeline from industrial perspectives from architectural coordination, massive data curation, to full-stack training recipes, inference & deployment, and the comprehensive evaluation for truly native modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态建模代表了从模态无关推理向世界建模迈进的关键一步。早期方法主要依赖后期融合，即通过输出头将编码器和冻结的语言骨干组装在一起；而最近的研究已将范式转向原生多模态建模（NMM），通过模态的内在整合实现更优的多模态性能。尽管潜力巨大，原生架构的设计空间仍未得到充分定义。本文为社区提供了一个形式化的路线图来指导这一转变。具体而言，我们明确定义了架构原生性，将中期融合和早期融合与非原生范式区分开来。我们进一步通过输入-输出对偶性的视角，将现有原生模型组织为三类：(i) 多到文本——仅输出文本的跨模态理解；(ii) 多到目标——面向场景的生成，如图像、音频和视频生成；(iii) 多到多——对称输入-输出的统一建模。我们对迈向最终NMM框架的转变进行了全面而工业级的调研，在该框架中，理解和生成在统一Transformer范式中无缝共存。我们从工业视角系统拆解了端到端流水线，涵盖架构协调、大规模数据策展、全栈训练配方、推理与部署，以及真正原生建模的全面评估。

</details>


### [105] [ERNIE-Image Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25347)
*Jiaxiang Liu,Zhida Feng,Pengyu Zou,Zhenyu Qian,Tianrui Zhu,Jun Xia,Yuehu Dong,Yanzheng Lin,Honglin Xiong,Anqi Chen,Yunpeng Ding,Jinghui Duan,Lin Gao,Chao Han,Tiechao He,Jiakang Hu,Ranjun Hua,Xueming Jiang,Qingli Kong,Yuting Lei,Tianyu Li,Yunlin Liu,Changling Liu,Yaxin Liu,Yi Liu,Xuguang Liu,Xiaolong Ma,Yan Pan,Yiran Ren,Nan Sheng,Yu Sun,Siyang Sun,Yixiang Tu,Yang Wan,Huanai Wang,Siqi Wang,Yang Wu,Youzhi Yang,Xiaowen Yang,Jianwen Yang,Yehua Yang,Quanwen Zhang,Xinmin Zhang,Haoxin Zhang,Xiang Zhang,Jun Zhang,Qian Zhang,Qiao Zhao,Qi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ERNIE-Image是一个基于8B单流DiT架构的开源文生图模型，通过自底向上的预训练数据管道降低噪声、保留长尾知识，以及自顶向下的后训练数据管道结合稳定DPO对齐人类审美，实现了接近顶级闭源模型的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 缩小当前开源文生图模型与领先闭源系统之间的性能差距。

Method: 预训练阶段采用自底向上的数据构建管道（细粒度图像分类、丰富字幕标注、美学评估、分层采样）；后训练阶段采用自顶向下数据管道（高需求场景多样化提示标注、稳定DPO对齐审美）；训练高效的ERNIE-Image-Turbo（8-NFE）并引入MT-DMD缓解蒸馏能力漂移；配备轻量级Prompt Enhancer将简洁用户意图扩展为结构化描述；开发工业级美学模型ERNIE-Image-Aes及人工标注基准ERNIE-Image-Aes-1K。

Result: 在指令跟随、文本渲染和美学质量方面，ERNIE-Image在开源模型中达到领先水平，接近顶级商业模型；ERNIE-Image-Turbo实现高效8-NFE生成且性能保持良好。

Conclusion: ERNIE-Image通过更有效的大规模预训练数据挖掘和训练过程中的监督质量提升，有效弥合了开源与闭源文生图模型之间的差距，并开源模型及美学资源以推动AIGC社区发展。

Original Abstract: We introduce ERNIE-Image, an open-source text-to-image generation model built upon an 8B single-stream DiT architecture. ERNIE-Image aims to bridge the gap between current open-source models and leading closed-source systems through more effective mining of large-scale pre-training data and improved supervision quality throughout training. During pre-training, we adopt a bottom-up data construction pipeline that combines fine-grained image categorization, rich caption annotation, aesthetic assessment, and hierarchical sampling. This strategy reduces data noise while preserving long-tail concepts and detailed real-world knowledge, providing a stronger foundation for complex generation tasks. In the post-training stage, we use a top-down data construction pipeline for high-demand scenarios, diversify prompt annotations to better match real user inputs, and apply a stabilized DPO strategy to align the model with human aesthetic preferences. We further train ERNIE-Image-Turbo for efficient 8-NFE generation and propose MT-DMD to mitigate capability drift during distillation. To make the model easier to use in practical scenarios, we equip it with a lightweight Prompt Enhancer that expands concise user intents into structured visual descriptions. In addition, we develop ERNIE-Image-Aes, an industrial-grade aesthetic model, together with ERNIE-Image-Aes-1K, a human-annotated benchmark for realistic aesthetic evaluation. Extensive qualitative and quantitative experiments show that ERNIE-Image achieves leading performance among open-source models and approaches top-tier commercial models in instruction following, text rendering, and aesthetic quality. We release the trained models and aesthetic resources to facilitate further academic research and technical progress in the AIGC community.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了ERNIE-Image，一个基于8B单流DiT架构的开源文生图模型。ERNIE-Image旨在通过更有效地挖掘大规模预训练数据以及在整个训练过程中提升监督质量，来弥合当前开源模型与领先闭源系统之间的差距。在预训练阶段，我们采用了一种自底向上的数据构建管道，结合了细粒度图像分类、丰富字幕标注、美学评估和分层采样。该策略在降低数据噪声的同时保留了长尾概念和详细的真实世界知识，为复杂生成任务提供了更强的基础。在后训练阶段，我们针对高需求场景采用自顶向下的数据构建管道，多样化提示标注以更好地匹配真实用户输入，并应用稳定DPO策略使模型与人类审美偏好对齐。我们还进一步训练了ERNIE-Image-Turbo以实现高效的8-NFE生成，并提出了MT-DMD以缓解蒸馏过程中的能力漂移。为使模型更易于在实际场景中使用，我们为其配备了一个轻量级的Prompt Enhancer，将简洁的用户意图扩展为结构化的视觉描述。此外，我们开发了工业级美学模型ERNIE-Image-Aes，以及人工标注的基准数据集ERNIE-Image-Aes-1K，用于现实场景的美学评估。大量定性和定量实验表明，ERNIE-Image在开源模型中取得了领先性能，并在指令跟随、文本渲染和美学质量方面接近顶级商业模型。我们发布了训练好的模型和美学资源，以促进AIGC社区的进一步学术研究和技术进步。

</details>


### [106] [Can MLLMs Reason Beyond Language? VisReason: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Vision-Centric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25364)
*Longteng Guo,Yifan Wang,Pengkang Huo,Tailai Chen,Yuze Wu,Jing Liu,Xinxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了VisReason基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在视觉中心推理（而非语言辅助推理）方面的能力，包含1,505个问题、10个类别，实验表明现有模型在该基准上表现远逊于人类，且测试时推理策略提升有限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs在视觉推理基准上表现强劲，但尚不清楚其推理是否真正基于视觉证据，而非依赖语言捷径或虚假关联。作者旨在构建一个视觉中心推理的评测基准，以严格区分视觉推理与语言辅助推理。

Method: 构建VisReason基准数据集，包含1,505个问题，覆盖感知推理、结构推理和概念推理三大类共10个子类别。问题设计强调感知与推理紧密耦合，排除仅靠语言知识即可回答的样本。

Result: VisReason揭示了人类与当前MLLMs之间的显著差距；现有模型在该基准上表现远不如在传统视觉推理基准上；测试时推理策略（如思维链）带来的提升有限，说明该基准对视觉中心推理具有独特的诊断价值。

Conclusion: VisReason是一个聚焦视觉中心推理的诊断性基准，能够有效区分视觉推理与语言辅助推理，暴露当前MLLMs在真正基于视觉证据的推理方面的不足。

Original Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on visual reasoning benchmarks, yet it remains unclear to what extent such performance reflects reasoning directly grounded in visual evidence. We introduce VisReason, a benchmark for vision-centric reasoning in everyday scenarios where perception and inference are tightly coupled. VisReason contains 1,505 questions across 10 categories spanning perceptual, structural, and conceptual reasoning. Our evaluation shows that VisReason poses a qualitatively different challenge from existing benchmarks, exposing substantial gaps between humans and current MLLMs and revealing limited benefits from test-time reasoning strategies. VisReason offers a focused diagnostic for evaluating vision-centric reasoning beyond language.

中文摘要翻译: 最近的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉推理基准上取得了强劲表现，但尚不清楚这种表现在多大程度上反映了直接基于视觉证据的推理。我们提出了VisReason，一个面向日常场景中视觉中心推理的基准，其中感知与推理紧密耦合。VisReason包含1,505个问题，覆盖感知推理、结构推理和概念推理三个大类共10个子类别。我们的评估表明，VisReason对现有基准构成了性质不同的挑战，揭示了人类与当前MLLMs之间的显著差距，并显示出测试时推理策略带来的收益十分有限。VisReason为评估超越语言的视觉中心推理提供了一个聚焦的诊断工具。

</details>


### [107] [Physics-Aware 3D Gaussian Editing for Driving Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25373)
*Feng Zhou,Jian Zhang,Yuhang Sun,He Wang,Qiong Wen,Debao Kong,Tieru Wu,Rui Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoVES是一种面向自动驾驶场景的物理感知3D高斯编辑系统，支持单图像驱动的道路几何插入（如减速带、凹陷路面），并耦合4-DOF半车动力学模型实现车辆位姿的垂直位移和俯仰校正。系统采用一次性无优化管线（全流程6.24秒），在Waymo数据集上实现了高效的物理感知驾驶场景生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3DGS场景编辑方法对道路几何编辑（如插入减速带、凹陷路面）支持有限，且未能将这些编辑与合理的车辆-道路交互动力学耦合。这对于生成极端驾驶场景的训练数据及评估系统在道路不规则条件下的可靠性至关重要。此外，现有优化方法需要分钟级逐编辑优化，而高效替代方法主要关注外观级或对象级操作，缺乏物理感知的道路不规则编辑能力。

Method: RoVES通过单图像驱动的道路几何插入管线（一次性、无优化），将编辑后的道路轮廓与4-DOF半车车辆动力学模型耦合，实现物理感知的车辆位姿校正（垂直位移和俯仰）。系统通过位姿编辑逐帧校正动态车辆位姿，以近似动力学一致的垂直位移和俯仰响应。全流程包括颜色迁移和基于车辆动力学的位姿校正。

Result: 在Waymo数据集上的实验表明，RoVES实现了实用的编辑效率（道路元素插入1.84秒，全流程6.24秒）和具有竞争力的视觉一致性，可用于物理感知的驾驶场景生成。

Conclusion: RoVES为自动驾驶场景提供了一种高效、物理感知的道路与车辆联合编辑方案，填补了现有3DGS编辑方法在道路不规则性编辑和车辆-道路交互动力学耦合方面的空白。

Original Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has shown great potential in autonomous driving simulation and data generation, enabling photorealistic reconstruction and flexible scene manipulation. However, existing 3DGS scene editing methods have limited support for road geometry editing (e.g., inserting speed humps or sunken roads), and generally do not couple such edits with plausible vehicle-road interaction dynamics. Such editing is essential for generating training data under extreme driving scenarios or evaluating system reliability under these road irregularities. Moreover, many optimization-based methods require minutes of per-edit refinement, while existing efficient alternatives mainly focus on appearance-level or object-level manipulation rather than physics-aware road irregularity editing. To address these limitations, we propose RoVES, a Road-and-Vehicle Editing System for physics-aware 3D Gaussian editing in driving scenes. RoVES enables single-image-driven road geometry insertion and couples the edited road profile with a 4-DOF half-car vehicle dynamics model to achieve physics-aware vehicle pose correction in vertical displacement and pitch. RoVES inserts road elements in a one-shot, optimization-free pipeline (1.84s), and the full pipeline (including color transfer and vehicle-dynamics-based pose correction) completes in 6.24s; it edits dynamic vehicles via pose editing and corrects poses frame-by-frame to approximate dynamics-consistent vertical displacement and pitch responses. Experiments on the Waymo dataset show that RoVES provides practical efficiency and competitive visual consistency for physics-aware driving scene generation.

中文摘要翻译: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）在自动驾驶仿真和数据生成领域展现出巨大潜力，能够实现照片级真实感的重建和灵活的场景编辑。然而，现有的3DGS场景编辑方法对道路几何编辑（如插入减速带或凹陷路面）支持有限，且通常未能将这些编辑与合理的车辆-道路交互动力学相耦合。这类编辑对于在极端驾驶场景下生成训练数据或评估系统在这些道路不规则条件下的可靠性至关重要。此外，许多基于优化的方法需要分钟级的逐编辑优化，而现有的高效替代方案主要关注外观级或对象级操作，而非物理感知的道路不规则编辑。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了RoVES——一种面向驾驶场景中物理感知3D高斯编辑的道路与车辆联合编辑系统。RoVES支持单图像驱动的道路几何插入，并将编辑后的道路轮廓与4自由度半车车辆动力学模型耦合，实现车辆位姿在垂直位移和俯仰方向上的物理感知校正。RoVES以一次性、无需优化的管线插入道路元素（1.84秒），完整管线（包括颜色迁移和基于车辆动力学的位姿校正）在6.24秒内完成；它通过位姿编辑编辑动态车辆，并逐帧校正位姿以逼近动力学一致的垂直位移和俯仰响应。在Waymo数据集上的实验表明，RoVES为物理感知的驾驶场景生成提供了实用的效率和具有竞争力的视觉一致性。

</details>


### [108] [Adversarial Orthogonal Disentanglement for LVLM Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25377)
*Ruoxi Cheng,Haoxuan Ma,Zhengfei Hai,Yiyan Huang,Ranjie Duan,Tianle Zhang,Xu Yang,Ziyi Ye,Xingjun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出对抗性正交解缠（AOD）框架，通过学习幻觉相关方向并采用无训练的双前向对比解码策略，有效抑制大型视觉-语言模型中的幻觉现象，同时保持通用能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大型视觉-语言模型存在幻觉问题（生成内容与视觉事实冲突），现有方法要么依赖昂贵的外部干预（如指令微调、检索），要么使用受限于有缺陷注意力权重和纠缠隐藏表示的内部机制，缺乏高效且通用的解决方案。

Method: 提出对抗性正交解缠（AOD）框架：通过最小化-最大化目标学习幻觉相关方向——分类器将幻觉信号集中到投影分量，对抗器通过梯度反转层从正交残差空间中移除幻觉信号；利用学到的方向实现无需训练的双前向对比解码策略，在抑制幻觉的同时保持通用能力。

Result: 在三个LVLM上、四个幻觉基准和四个实用性基准的实验中，AOD一致优于强基线：POPE准确率平均提升6%以上，AMBER提升6%，在MMMU等实用性任务上保持强性能。鲁棒性分析表明AOD捕获的是通用幻觉偏差而非数据集特定伪影。

Conclusion: AOD作为一种潜在几何框架，通过解缠幻觉相关信号实现了高效、无需训练干预的幻觉缓解，且具有良好的跨数据集迁移能力，为提升LVLM可靠性提供了新途径。

Original Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have advanced multimodal understanding, yet their reliability is limited by hallucination, where generated content conflicts with visual facts. Existing mitigation methods either rely on costly external interventions, such as instruction tuning and retrieval, or use internal mechanisms that remain limited by flawed attention weights and entangled hidden representations. We propose Adversarial Orthogonal Disentanglement (AOD), a latent geometric framework for mitigating LVLM hallucinations. AOD learns a hallucination-related direction through a minimax objective: a classifier concentrates hallucination signals into the projected component, while an adversary removes them from the orthogonal residual space via a Gradient Reversal Layer. The learned direction enables a training-free dual-forward-pass contrastive decoding strategy that suppresses hallucinations while preserving general capabilities. Experiments on three LVLMs across four hallucination and four utility benchmarks show that AOD consistently outperforms strong baselines. It improves POPE accuracy by over 6\% on average, boosts AMBER by 6\%, and maintains strong performance on utility tasks such as MMMU. Further analysis shows robust transfer across datasets, suggesting that AOD captures general hallucination-related biases rather than dataset-specific artifacts. Our source code and datasets are available at https://github.com/Hunter-Wrynn/AOD.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）在多模态理解方面取得了进展，但其可靠性受到幻觉问题的限制，即生成内容与视觉事实相冲突。现有的缓解方法要么依赖昂贵的外部干预（如指令微调和检索），要么使用内部机制，但这些机制仍受限于有缺陷的注意力权重和纠缠的隐藏表示。我们提出对抗性正交解缠（Adversarial Orthogonal Disentanglement, AOD），这是一种用于缓解LVLM幻觉的潜在几何框架。AOD通过最小化-最大化目标学习一个与幻觉相关的方向：分类器将幻觉信号集中到投影分量中，而对抗器通过梯度反转层（Gradient Reversal Layer）将其从正交残差空间中移除。学到的方向使得一种无需训练的双前向对比解码策略成为可能，该策略在抑制幻觉的同时保持通用能力。在三个LVLM上跨四个幻觉基准和四个实用性基准的实验表明，AOD一致性地优于强基线。它平均将POPE准确率提升6%以上，将AMBER提升6%，并在MMMU等实用性任务上保持强性能。进一步分析显示其具有稳健的跨数据集迁移能力，表明AOD捕获的是通用的幻觉相关偏差而非数据集特定的伪影。我们的源代码和数据集可从 https://github.com/Hunter-Wrynn/AOD 获取。

</details>


### [109] [CollectionLoRA: Collecting 50 Effects in 1 LoRA via Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25378)
*Fangtai Wu,Hailong Guo,Shijie Huang,Jiayi Song,Yubo Huang,Mushui Liu,Zhao Wang,Yunlong Yu,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CollectionLoRA提出了一种多教师同策略蒸馏框架，能够将多达50种不同的效果LoRA以及少步生成能力蒸馏到单个LoRA中，从根本上解决参数干扰问题并大幅降低部署成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有定制化图像编辑方法中，随着所需视觉效果数量增长，存储和动态加载大量效果LoRA会导致部署开销显著增加；此外，将效果LoRA与加速模块级联使用时会产生严重的参数干扰，导致概念泄露和风格退化。

Method: (1) 概率双流路由机制——训练时随机切换数据源，增强模型在未见场景中的泛化能力；(2) 非对称正交提示策略——在提示空间中实现概念隔离；(3) 由粗到细的蒸馏目标——缓解教师与学生模型之间的分布差异。

Result: 实验表明，CollectionLoRA能够将所有定制化效果和少步生成能力蒸馏到单个LoRA中，在降低部署开销的同时，实现了与独立训练教师模型相当或更优的概念保真度。

Conclusion: CollectionLoRA通过多教师蒸馏框架有效解决了多效果LoRA集成时的参数干扰问题，显著降低了部署成本，且保持了良好的概念保真度。

Original Abstract: Customized image editing aims to equip pre-trained diffusion models with specific visual effects using limited paired data, typically via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). As the number of desired effects grows, storing and dynamically loading numerous these effect LoRAs significantly increases deployment overhead. Furthermore, current pipelines typically cascade these effect LoRAs with acceleration modules for fast generation, which triggers severe parameter interference and results in concept bleeding and style degradation. We propose CollectionLoRA, a multi-teacher on-policy distillation framework capable of distilling the concepts of up to 50 different effect LoRAs along with few-step generation capabilities into a single LoRA. This fundamentally resolves the feature interference issue and significantly reduces deployment costs. Specifically, the method introduces (i) a Probabilistic Dual-Stream Routing mechanism that enables the model to randomly switch between data sources during training, effectively enhancing its generalization in unseen scenarios; (ii) an Asymmetric Orthogonal Prompting strategy to achieve concept isolation within the prompt space; (iii) a Coarse-to-Fine Distillation Objective to mitigate the distribution gap between the teacher and student models. Extensive evaluations show that CollectionLoRA distills all customized effects and few-step generation into a single LoRA, reducing deployment overhead while achieving concept fidelity comparable to or better than independently trained teacher models.

中文摘要翻译: 定制化图像编辑旨在利用有限的配对数据，通过低秩适配（LoRA）为预训练扩散模型配备特定的视觉效果。随着所需效果数量的增加，存储和动态加载大量效果LoRA会显著增加部署开销。此外，现有流程通常将这些效果LoRA与加速模块级联使用以实现快速生成，这会引发严重的参数干扰，导致概念泄露和风格退化。我们提出CollectionLoRA，一种多教师同策略蒸馏框架，能够将多达50种不同效果LoRA的概念以及少步生成能力蒸馏到单个LoRA中。这从根本上解决了特征干扰问题，并显著降低了部署成本。具体而言，该方法引入了：(i) 概率双流路由机制，使模型在训练过程中能够在数据源之间随机切换，有效增强其在未见场景中的泛化能力；(ii) 非对称正交提示策略，在提示空间中实现概念隔离；(iii) 由粗到细的蒸馏目标，以缓解教师与学生模型之间的分布差异。大量评估表明，CollectionLoRA将所有定制化效果和少步生成蒸馏到单个LoRA中，在降低部署开销的同时，实现了与独立训练的教师模型相当或更优的概念保真度。

</details>


### [110] [Subspace-Guided Semantic and Topological Invariant Registration for Annotation-Free Ultrasound Plane Quality Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25396)
*Chunzheng Zhu,Jianxin Lin,Feng Wang,Cheng Jiang,Guanghua Tan,Zhenyu Zhou,Shengli Li,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STRIQ是一个无需标注的超声图像质量控制框架，通过潜空间配准对齐和正交知识子空间分解，实现与临床质量评分高度相关的自动化质量评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有超声图像质量控制方法严重依赖逐平面标注，或使用伪标签方法，后者在临床采集固有的空间形变下容易产生系统性偏差。因此，亟需一种无需标注、鲁棒且可靠的超声图像质量控制方法。

Method: 提出STRIQ框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 潜空间配准对齐器(LRA)，通过方差谱准则从无标签数据中自主蒸馏结构稳定的锚点，建立查询图像与锚点之间的层次化特征空间对应关系；(2) 正交知识子空间(OKS)模块，将平面特定表示分解为相互正交的子空间，实现细粒度专家协作并防止平面间干扰，从而基于子空间邻近度进行质量度量。

Result: 在内部US4QA和公开CAMUS数据集上的大量实验表明，STRIQ与临床质量评分达到了最先进的相关性。

Conclusion: STRIQ建立了一种无需标注、实时可靠的超声质量控制新范式。

Original Abstract: Reliable quality control (QC) of ultrasound images is essential for both real-time acquisition guidance and retrospective clinical audit, yet existing approaches rely heavily on per-plane annotations, or employ pseudo-labeling prone to systematic bias under spatial deformations inherent in clinical acquisition. We present STRIQ, a registration-driven framework that recasts annotation-free US plane quality control as a subspace-guided consistency measurement problem. Specifically, STRIQ introduces a Latent Registration Aligner (LRA) to establish hierarchical feature space correspondences between query images and variance-driven anchors, which are autonomously distilled from unlabeled data via a variance spectrum criterion to serve as structurally stable prototypes. To further disambiguate anatomical planes and mitigate negative knowledge transfer, we propose an Orthogonal Knowledge Subspace (OKS) module. The OKS decomposes plane-specific representations into mutually orthogonal subspaces, enabling fine-grained expert collaboration while preventing inter-plane interference, ensuring that the quality metric is grounded in principled subspace proximity. Extensive experiments on the in-house US4QA and public CAMUS datasets demonstrate that STRIQ achieves state-of-the-art correlation with clinical quality scores, establishing a new paradigm for annotation-free, real-time reliable ultrasound quality control. Our code is available at https://github.com/zhcz328/STRIQ.

中文摘要翻译: 超声图像可靠的质量控制（QC）对于实时采集引导和回顾性临床审核至关重要，然而现有方法严重依赖逐平面标注，或使用在临床采集固有空间形变下容易产生系统性偏差的伪标签方法。我们提出了STRIQ，一个基于配准的框架，将无需标注的超声平面质量控制重新定义为子空间引导的一致性度量问题。具体而言，STRIQ引入了一个潜空间配准对齐器（LRA），在查询图像与方差驱动的锚点之间建立层次化特征空间对应关系，这些锚点通过方差谱准则从无标签数据中自主蒸馏而来，作为结构稳定的原型。为了进一步消除解剖平面的歧义并缓解负向知识迁移，我们提出了一个正交知识子空间（OKS）模块。OKS将平面特定表示分解为相互正交的子空间，在实现细粒度专家协作的同时防止平面间干扰，确保质量度量基于原则性的子空间邻近度。在内部US4QA和公开CAMUS数据集上的大量实验表明，STRIQ与临床质量评分达到了最先进的相关性，建立了一种无需标注、实时可靠的超声质量控制新范式。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/zhcz328/STRIQ 获取。

</details>


### [111] [Anatomy-Anchored Self-Supervision: Distilling Vision Foundation Models for Invariant Ultrasound Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25402)
*Chunzheng Zhu,Yijun Wang,Jianxin Lin,Feng Wang,Hongwei Wang,Lei Zhao,Shengli Li,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种以解剖锚点驱动的超声图像自监督预训练框架ANAUS，通过LP-SAM实现无标注解剖结构分割，并设计双策略自监督学习范式，在6个公开数据集上超越现有方法，兼顾临床部署的计算效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有超声图像自监督方法仅在图像或帧级别操作，忽略了与临床对齐的解剖结构上下文信息，导致学到的表示缺乏临床意义的解剖语义。

Method: (1) 设计LP-SAM模块，利用可学习的潜在提示引擎结合一次性域自适应，实现无标注的解剖结构分割；(2) 提出双策略自监督学习范式：视图间语义感知的解剖分离对齐（增强同一解剖区域特征不变性，促进不同结构区分性）和上下文核心区域预测（迫使模型重建被破坏区域，捕获细粒度结构细节）。

Result: 在6个公开数据集上的广泛评估表明，ANAUS持续超越当前最先进方法，同时保持临床部署所需的计算效率。

Conclusion: 将自监督表示学习从通用视觉区域转向临床有意义的解剖结构，能够显著提升超声图像表征质量，且无需额外标注即可规模化实现解剖感知学习。

Original Abstract: Self-supervised pre-training paradigm has gained increasing prominence for learning transferable representations in medical imaging, yet existing methods for ultrasound (US) images operate at the image or frame level, overlooking the anatomical context for clinical-aligned representation learning. In this work, we propose an anatomy-anchored ultrasound self-supervision framework ANAUS that shifts representation learning from generic visual regions to clinically meaningful anatomical structures. Utilizing a learnable latent prompt engine alongside a one-time domain adaptation on existing public image--mask pairs, we empower the LP-SAM module to achieve annotation-free anatomy delineation at scale. Building upon this anatomical grounding, we propose a dual-policy self-supervised learning paradigm consisting of inter-view semantics-aware anatomy-separating alignment and contextual core-region prediction to enhance representation learning. Specifically, the former enforces feature invariance within identical anatomical regions while promoting discriminability across distinct structures; the latter compels the model to reconstruct corrupted regions, thereby capturing fine-grained structural details. Extensive evaluations on six public datasets demonstrate that \ours{} consistently outstrips current state-of-the-art methods while maintaining the computational efficiency essential for clinical deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/zhcz328/ANAUS.

中文摘要翻译: 自监督预训练范式在医学影像中学习可迁移表征方面日益受到重视，然而现有的超声图像方法仅在图像或帧级别操作，忽视了用于临床对齐表示学习的解剖结构上下文。本文提出了一种解剖锚定的超声自监督框架ANAUS，将表示学习从通用视觉区域转向临床有意义的解剖结构。该框架利用可学习的潜在提示引擎结合一次性域自适应于已有的公开图像-掩码对，使LP-SAM模块能够实现无标注的大规模解剖结构分割。基于这一解剖锚定，我们提出了一种双策略自监督学习范式，包括视图间语义感知的解剖分离对齐和上下文核心区域预测，以增强表示学习。前者在相同解剖区域内强制特征不变性，同时提升不同结构间的区分性；后者迫使模型重建被破坏的区域，从而捕获细粒度的结构细节。在6个公开数据集上的广泛评估表明，ANAUS持续超越当前最先进的方法，同时保持了临床部署所需的计算效率。代码已开源：https://github.com/zhcz328/ANAUS。

</details>


### [112] [Towards Active Real-to-Twin Inspection: A New Paradigm for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25407)
*Jiaxuan Liu,Yunkang Cao,Yufeng Chen,Chunyang Li,Yuhuan Du,Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Real-to-Twin异常检测新任务及AVATAR框架，通过在无缺陷的真实与CAD数字孪生对之间学习鲁棒的语义对齐，实现零样本、动态视角下的异常检测，显著优于现有基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的零样本异常检测依赖被动、固定视角的2D图像，无法适应真实工业检测中主动、动态的观测需求，严重制约了在实体工业检测中的应用。

Method: 提出AVATAR框架，利用仅含无缺陷样本的真实与CAD数字孪生对，学习二者间的鲁棒语义对齐，弥合良性的Sim2Real域差异；通过将CAD先验转化为动态、无异常的参考，以零样本方式将异常定位为不可对齐的偏差，无需缺陷标注。

Result: 大量实验表明，AVATAR在性能上大幅优于经过适应的现有最佳基线方法，且对剧烈的视角变化展现出卓越的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过构建真实与数字孪生之间的语义对齐，AVATAR有效突破了固定视角的限制，实现了零样本、动态环境下的工业异常检测，具有显著的应用潜力。

Original Abstract: The deployment of zero-shot anomaly detection (AD) in embodied industrial inspection is severely bottlenecked by its reliance on passive, fixed-viewpoint 2D imagery. Such formulations inherently fail to accommodate the active, dynamic observations required in real-world environments. To break this limitation, we introduce Real-to-Twin Anomaly Detection, a novel task that evaluates physical observations directly against geometrically matched CAD Digital Twins. To tackle this new task, we propose AVATAR, a framework designed to learn robust semantic alignment between Real and Digital Twins. By bridging benign Sim2Real domain gaps using only defect-free pairs, AVATAR effectively transforms CAD priors into dynamic, anomaly-free references. This elegant formulation enables the model to localize diverse anomalies in a zero-shot manner as unalignable deviations, eliminating the need for defect annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AVATAR substantially outperforms adapted state-of-the-art baselines, exhibiting exceptional robustness to severe viewpoint variations. The code and dataset will be made publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 零样本异常检测在实体工业检测中的部署受到其依赖被动、固定视角的2D图像的严重瓶颈。这类方法本质上无法适应真实环境中所需的主动、动态观测。为突破这一限制，我们提出了Real-to-Twin异常检测（真实到孪生异常检测），这是一个评估物理观测与几何匹配的CAD数字孪生之间差异的新任务。针对这一新任务，我们提出了AVATAR框架，旨在学习真实与数字孪生之间的鲁棒语义对齐。通过仅使用无缺陷样本对弥合良性的Sim2Real域差异，AVATAR有效地将CAD先验转化为动态、无异常的参考。这一简洁的设计使得模型能够以零样本方式将多样化异常定位为不可对齐的偏差，无需缺陷标注。大量实验表明，AVATAR大幅优于经过适应的现有最佳基线方法，并对剧烈的视角变化展现出卓越的鲁棒性。代码和数据集将公开发布。

</details>


### [113] [Binding Visual Features Point by Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25427)
*Udith Haputhanthri,Declan Campbell,Rim Assouel,Jonathan D. Cohen,Taylor W. Webb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉语言模型在多目标场景中的绑定问题，发现通过文本引导的"指向"机制能够诱导内部视觉搜索过程，从而消除绑定错误并实现组合泛化，验证了串行处理解决绑定问题的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉语言模型在处理多目标场景时存在持续失败，表现为物体特征在上下文中无法准确绑定（即"绑定问题"）。最近提出的"指向"方法（使用显式空间坐标引用物体）能提升多目标任务性能，但其背后的机制原理以及与人类视觉串行处理的关系尚不清楚。本文旨在探究这种改进的机理层面原因。

Method: 通过分析"指向"机制如何影响模型内部表示，研究学习指向行为是否诱导出类似视觉搜索的串行处理过程；通过微调将指向行为泛化到新任务，并评估其对绑定错误和组合泛化的影响。

Result: 学习通过文本进行指向能够诱导出内部视觉搜索程序；指向行为可通过微调泛化到新任务，消除绑定错误并实现组合泛化。

Conclusion: 串行处理能够像解决生物视觉中的绑定问题一样，有效解决视觉语言模型的绑定问题，为模型提供了原理性验证。

Original Abstract: Despite success on standard benchmarks, vision language models display persistent failures on tasks involving processing of multi-object scenes, including many tasks that are relatively easy for humans. Recent work has found that these failures may stem from a basic inability to accurately bind object features in-context, a challenge that is referred to as the "binding problem" in cognitive science and neuroscience. The human visual system is thought to solve this binding problem via serial processing, attending to individual objects one at a time so as to avoid interference from other objects. Recent work has proposed "pointing" -- the use of explicit spatial coordinates to refer to objects -- as an analogous solution for vision language models, and found that it improves performance on challenging multi-object tasks. However, it is unclear $\textit{why}$ (i.e., on a mechanistic or representational level) this approach improves performance, and how directly this relates to serial processing in human vision. Here, we investigate this question. We find that learning to point-via-text induces an internal visual search routine, and we characterize the mechanisms that support this procedure. We also find that pointing behavior can be generalized to new tasks via fine-tuning, and that doing so eliminates binding errors and enables compositional generalization. These results provide a proof-of-principle that serial processing can solve the binding problem for vision language models just as it does for biological vision.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管在标准基准测试上表现成功，视觉语言模型在处理涉及多目标场景的任务时仍存在持续性失败，包括许多对人类来说相对容易的任务。最近的研究发现，这些失败可能源于模型无法在上下文语境中准确绑定物体特征，这一问题在认知科学和神经科学中被称为"绑定问题"。人类视觉系统被认为通过串行处理来解决绑定问题，即一次只关注一个单独物体，以避免来自其他物体的干扰。最近的研究提出"指向"——使用显式的空间坐标来指代物体——作为视觉语言模型的一种类比解决方案，并发现它能提升具有挑战性的多目标任务上的性能。然而，这种方法为何（即在机制或表征层面上）能提升性能，以及它与人类视觉中串行处理之间有何直接关联，尚不清楚。在此，我们研究了这些问题。我们发现，学习通过文本进行指向会诱导出内部视觉搜索程序，并且我们刻画了支持这一过程的相关机制。我们还发现，指向行为可以通过微调泛化到新任务，这样做能够消除绑定错误并实现组合泛化。这些结果提供了一个原理性验证：串行处理能够像解决生物视觉中的绑定问题一样，有效解决视觉语言模型的绑定问题。

</details>


### [114] [Does Seeing More Mean Knowing More? Mono-Anchored Advantage Normalization for Multi-Source Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25437)
*Fanhu Zeng,Zhicong Luo,Zefan Wang,You Li,Chi Chen,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MARS框架，通过单模态奖励锚定多源融合中的信息增益，解决多源视觉推理中源间干扰导致的性能下降问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将多源输入视为信息简单叠加，缺乏区分信息增益与干扰的机制，尤其在物理属性和语义差异大的模态（如红外与深度）上表现不如单源推理。

Method: 提出MARS框架：以单模态奖励作为动态锚点，将多源融合的信息增益显式纳入优势归一化，自适应增强源间相互促进、抑制噪声/冲突，实现梯度估计中信息增益的量化与模态一致性调控。

Result: 在GRPO和DAPO上分别取得3.2%和4.9%的性能提升，跨数据集验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析和实验证明，基于单模态锚定的多源推理框架能有效量化并利用多源融合的信息增益，实现优于单源推理的性能。

Original Abstract: Visual reasoning through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has achieved remarkable progress. However, when dealing with multi-source inputs, existing approaches tend to treat them as a mere accumulation of information, lacking explicit mechanisms to distinguish whether integrating additional sources yields information gain or introduces interference. Therefore, they struggle to effectively model dynamic interaction when integrating multiple sources, particularly when they differ significantly in physical properties and semantics, e.g., infrared and depth, leading to inferior performance to mono-source reasoning when a certain source holds the dominant signal. To address this issue, we propose MARS, a novel mono-anchored multi-source reasoning framework that models each visual modality as an independent information source. Specifically, by treating mono-source rewards as dynamic anchors, our method explicitly incorporates the information gain introduced by multi-source fusion into advantage normalization and adaptively emphasizes mutual promotion between sources while suppressing potential noise or conflicts during RLVR. From theoretical analysis, our method effectively quantifies information gain introduced by multi-source integration in gradient estimation, enabling consistent modality regulation. Empirical results also show impressive 3.2% and 4.9% performance gains on GRPO and DAPO across diverse datasets, confirming effectiveness of our method.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在视觉推理中取得了显著进展。然而，在处理多源输入时，现有方法倾向于将其视为信息的简单累加，缺乏明确的机制来区分整合额外源是带来信息增益还是引入干扰。因此，它们难以有效建模多源融合时的动态交互，特别是当多源在物理属性和语义上差异显著时（例如红外与深度），会导致在某一源承载主导信号时性能反而低于单源推理。为解决这一问题，我们提出MARS，一种新颖的单锚定多源推理框架，将每种视觉模态建模为独立的信息源。具体而言，通过将单源奖励视为动态锚点，我们的方法显式地将多源融合引入的信息增益纳入优势归一化，并在RLVR过程中自适应地增强源间的相互促进，同时抑制潜在的噪声或冲突。理论分析表明，我们的方法有效量化了梯度估计中多源整合引入的信息增益，实现了模态的一致性调控。实验结果表明，在GRPO和DAPO上，该方法在多种数据集上分别取得了3.2%和4.9%的性能提升，证实了方法的有效性。

</details>


### [115] [Pantheon360: Taming Digital Twin Generation via 3D-Aware 360° Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25449)
*Ting-Hsuan Chen,Ying-Huan Chen,Tao Tu,Jie-Ying Lee,Cho-Ying Wu,Fangzhou Lin,Hengyuan Zhang,David Paz,Xinyu Huang,Yuliang Guo,Yu-Lun Liu,Yue Wang,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pantheon360提出一种基于3D感知的360°视频扩散框架，通过从稀疏360°输入中重建3D缓存作为几何骨架，结合扩散模型进行纹理细化，实现了高保真、几何一致的完整数字孪生生成，解决了透视视频生成器因视野有限导致的跨视角不一致和时间漂移问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 透视视频生成器受限于狭窄视场角（FoV），为覆盖全局场景需设计长轨迹或多视角轨迹，这放大了跨视角不一致性和时间漂移问题。作者认为360°视频生成能提供全景覆盖，简化轨迹设计并提供强全局上下文来维持一致性，因此探索用360°视频扩散实现数字孪生生成。

Method: 提出Pantheon360框架，核心思想是从稀疏360°输入中显式重建一个3D缓存（3D Cache），作为任意用户定义相机路径的几何骨架。扩散模型专注于逼真纹理细化，而3D缓存强制执行全局几何一致性，从而分离几何与纹理的优化任务。

Result: 实验表明，Pantheon360在视觉质量和几何一致性方面均达到优越性能，实现了可靠且灵活的360°场景生成，适用于下游模拟和数字孪生应用。

Conclusion: Pantheon360通过显式3D缓存与扩散模型的协同，有效解决了透视视频生成器在数字孪生任务中的视野受限和一致性问题，为360°场景的高保真生成提供了可靠方案。

Original Abstract: Generating complete digital twins from videos requires precise camera control, global scene coverage, and strict spatial-temporal consistency constraints that remain challenging for perspective video generators due to their limited field of view (FoV). Their narrow FoV forces long or multi-view trajectories, amplifying cross-view inconsistency and temporal drift. We argue that 360° video generation offers a natural solution: panoramic coverage simplifies trajectory design and provides a strong global context for maintaining coherence. We introduce Pantheon360: Taming Digital Twin Generation via 3D-Aware 360° Video Diffusion, a controllable 360° video generation framework that synthesizes high-fidelity videos from sparse 360° inputs. The key idea is an explicit 3D Cache, reconstructed from the input, which serves as a geometric scaffold for any user-defined camera path. This allows the diffusion model to focus on photorealistic texture refinement while the 3D Cache enforces global geometric consistency. Experiments show that Pantheon360 achieves superior visual quality and unmatched geometric coherence, enabling reliable and flexible 360° scene generation for downstream simulation and digital-twin applications.

中文摘要翻译: 从视频中生成完整的数字孪生需要精确的相机控制、全局场景覆盖以及严格的空间-时间一致性约束，这些对于透视视频生成器而言因其有限的视场角（FoV）而仍然具有挑战性。狭窄的FoV迫使设计长轨迹或多视角轨迹，放大了跨视角不一致性和时间漂移。我们认为，360°视频生成提供了一种自然的解决方案：全景覆盖简化了轨迹设计，并为维持一致性提供了强大的全局上下文。我们提出了Pantheon360：通过3D感知的360°视频扩散实现数字孪生生成，这是一个可控的360°视频生成框架，能够从稀疏的360°输入合成高保真视频。其关键思想是一个从输入中重建的显式3D缓存，它作为任意用户定义相机路径的几何骨架。这使得扩散模型能够专注于逼真的纹理细化，同时由3D缓存强制执行全局几何一致性。实验表明，Pantheon360实现了优越的视觉质量和无与伦比的几何一致性，为下游仿真和数字孪生应用提供了可靠且灵活的360°场景生成。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [7 Tesla Quantitative MRI and Machine Learning for Exploratory Motor Subtype Stratification and Diagnosis in Parkinson's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24179)
*Anne Louise Kristoffersen,Runa Geirmundsdatter Unsgård,Marc-Antoine Fortin,Ingrid Gylterud Kvålsgard,Kjersti Eline Stige,Thanh Pierre Doan,Erik Magnus Berntsen,Charalampos Tzoulis,Pål Erik Goa*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于深度学习的自动脑部分割（U-Net）结合7T MRI定量图谱，通过特征选择优化分类器，实现帕金森病（PD）及其亚型（PIGD与TD）的客观分层。结果表明，特征选择显著提升了分类性能，支持可解释的低维影像特征用于PD诊断和表型分层。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 帕金森病具有高度异质性，现有基于运动症状的亚型分类缺乏客观的影像学生物标志物。本研究旨在探索深度学习自动脑分割与7T MRI定量成像能否有效区分健康对照、姿势不稳/步态困难型（PIGD）和震颤主导型（TD）患者，并用于客观的PD分层。

Method: 纳入21名健康对照和24名帕金森患者。使用U-Net进行自动脑部分割（评估指标为DSC系数），从7T MRI提取定量图谱特征。定义两种分类策略（5折交叉验证）：方法A使用所有提取特征；方法B通过特征选择寻找最优特征子集。完成三项分类任务：（1）HC vs PwP；（2）PIGD vs TD；（3）多分类HC vs PIGD vs TD。

Result: U-Net训练平均DSC为0.86。方法A：任务1准确率0.69、AUC 0.73；任务2准确率0.69、AUC 0.90；任务3准确率0.62、AUC 0.66。方法B（特征选择后）：任务1准确率0.82、AUC 0.93；任务2准确率1.00、AUC 1.00；任务3准确率0.73、AUC 0.91。特征选择在所有任务上均显著提升了分类性能。

Conclusion: 基于深度学习的脑分割结合定量MRI特征选择，相比使用全部特征显著改善了PD诊断和亚型分类性能，证明可解释的低维影像学特征在PD诊断支持和表型分层方面具有潜力。未来需要在更大规模、多中心的研究中评估泛化性和稳定性。

Original Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) is a highly heterogeneous disease, including which motor symptoms are dominating. Imaging biomarkers that support subtype stratification could also improve biological understanding and study design, and enable personalized treatment strategies. This study evaluates whether deep-learning based automatic brain segmentation, in addition to quantitative maps from 7 Tesla MRI, can highlight differences between Healthy Controls (HC), Postural Instability and Gait Difficulty (PIGD) and Tremor Dominant (TD), and subsequently be used for objective PD stratification. The performance of machine learning classifiers may be improved with feature selection. 21 HC, and 24 people with PD (PwP) were included. The U-Net training was assessed with DSC. Two classification approaches using 5-fold cross-validation were defined across three tasks: (1) HC vs PwP; (2) PIGD vs TD; (3) multiclass, HC vs PIGD vs TD. Approach A used all extracted features. Approach B found the optimal subset of features for the classification tasks. The U-Net achieved mean DSC of 0.86 for all ROIs during training. Approach A: Task 1 best accuracy of 0.69 and best AUC of 0.73. Task 2 accuracy 0.69, AUC 0.90. Task 3 accuracy 0.62, AUC 0.66. Approach B: Task 1 accuracy of 0.82 and AUC of 0.93. Task 2 accuracy 1.00, AUC 1.00. Task 3 accuracy 0.73, AUC 0.91. DL-based segmentation combined with qMRI feature selection improved classification relative to using all features, supporting the potential of interpretable, low-dimensional imaging signatures for PD diagnosis support and phenotype stratification. Larger, multi-site studies are warranted to assess generalizability and stability.

中文摘要翻译: 帕金森病（PD）是一种高度异质性的疾病，包括运动症状的差异。支持亚型分层的影像生物标志物能够增进生物学理解、改善研究设计，并实现个性化治疗策略。本研究评估了基于深度学习的自动脑部分割结合7特斯拉MRI定量图谱，能否揭示健康对照（HC）、姿势不稳/步态困难型（PIGD）和震颤主导型（TD）之间的差异，并用于客观的PD分层。通过特征选择可以提升机器学习分类器的性能。研究纳入21名健康对照和24名帕金森患者（PwP）。U-Net训练采用Dice相似系数（DSC）评估。定义两种分类方法进行5折交叉验证，完成三项任务：（1）HC vs PwP；（2）PIGD vs TD；（3）多分类HC vs PIGD vs TD。方法A使用所有提取特征；方法B寻找最优特征子集。U-Net在所有感兴趣区域（ROI）的训练平均DSC为0.86。方法A：任务1最佳准确率0.69、最佳AUC 0.73；任务2准确率0.69、AUC 0.90；任务3准确率0.62、AUC 0.66。方法B：任务1准确率0.82、AUC 0.93；任务2准确率1.00、AUC 1.00；任务3准确率0.73、AUC 0.91。基于深度学习的分割结合定量MRI特征选择相比使用全部特征改善了分类性能，支持可解释的低维影像学特征在PD诊断支持和表型分层中的潜力。未来需要更大规模、多中心的研究来评估泛化性和稳定性。

</details>


### [117] [Causal Evaluation of Contributing Factors to Urban Heat Island](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24459)
*Sadaf Alavi Soltani,Saeid Niazmardi,Ali Esmaeily*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Hotelling T平方检验的统计方法，用于分析城市热岛（UHI）与其贡献因素之间的因果关系。通过对德黑兰22个市区2003-2021年的数据分析，发现所有区域的地表温度呈稳定或上升趋势，NDWI和NDBI的时间变化与UHI无因果关系，而其他因素（如植被覆盖和降水）的变化则加剧了UHI效应。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 城市热岛效应受多种因素复杂交互影响，且这些因素随时空变化，理解UHI与其贡献因素之间的因果关系对减轻其环境和健康影响至关重要。

Method: 基于Hotelling T平方检验的统计方法：先估计不同城区的UHI趋势，然后将城区分为UHI上升组和非上升组，比较两组在贡献因素上的差异，若差异显著则解释为存在因果关系。

Result: 德黑兰所有22个市区的地表温度呈稳定或上升趋势；NDWI和NDBI的时间变化与UHI无因果关系；而降水、NDSI、NDVI、EVI等因素的变化被识别为加剧UHI的贡献因素。

Conclusion: 所提出的Hotelling T平方检验方法能够有效识别UHI与其贡献因素之间的因果关系，不同因素对UHI的影响存在差异，针对性地控制相关因素有助于缓解城市热岛效应。

Original Abstract: Urban heat islands (UHI) are formed due to complex interactions between various factors. UHI, its contributing factors, and their interaction vary over time and location. Accordingly, understanding the causal relation between UHI and its contributing factors is essential to minimizing its adverse effects on the environment and human health. Here, we proposed a statistical method based on Hotelling's T-square test to analyze this association. The proposed test estimates the UHI trends across different urban districts and compares the UHI contributing factors between the districts with increasing and non-increasing UHI trends. This comparison, if significantly different, can be interpreted as evidence of a causal association between the factor and UHI. This research used the proposed test to analyze the UHI and its contributing factors across 22 municipal districts of Tehran between 2003 and 2021. We examined the time series of weather conditions (measured by precipitation, NDSI, and NDWI), vegetation cover (measured by NDVI and EVI), and urban density (measured by NDBI) as factors contributing to the UHI, which was measured through nighttime LST. The results showed that all districts in Tehran exhibited stable or increasing trends in LST, leading to UHI effects. The proposed test indicated that the temporal changes in NDWI and NDBI did not have a causal relationship with UHIs. Meanwhile, variations in other factors were identified as contributing to the intensification of UHIs.

中文摘要翻译: 城市热岛（UHI）是由多种因素间的复杂相互作用形成的。UHI及其贡献因素和它们的相互作用会随时间和地点变化。因此，理解UHI与其贡献因素之间的因果关系对于减轻其对环境和人类健康的不利影响至关重要。本文提出了一种基于Hotelling T平方检验的统计方法来分析这种关联。所提出的检验方法估算了不同城区的UHI趋势，并比较了UHI呈上升趋势和未呈上升趋势的城区之间的贡献因素差异。如果这种比较结果显著不同，则可解释为该因素与UHI之间存在因果关系的证据。本研究利用所提出的方法分析了2003年至2021年间德黑兰22个市区的UHI及其贡献因素。我们考察了天气条件（以降水量、NDSI和NDWI衡量）、植被覆盖（以NDVI和EVI衡量）和城市密度（以NDBI衡量）的时间序列作为UHI的贡献因素，其中UHI通过夜间地表温度（LST）测量。结果表明，德黑兰所有市区的地表温度均呈现稳定或上升趋势，导致UHI效应。所提出的检验表明，NDWI和NDBI的时间变化与UHI之间不存在因果关系，而其他因素的变化被识别为加剧UHI的贡献因素。

</details>


### [118] [A Signal Extraction Approach for Remote Heart Rate Variability Assessment Using Proxy Measure in a Driving Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24525)
*Đorđe D. Nešković,Nadica Miljković*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文评估了多种rPPG算法（2SR、CHROM、POS、PCA）在驾驶模拟器场景下基于超像素面部区域（10和20区域）的心率监测性能，并提出了两种新的峰值增强方法（Lp范数和分数阶导数FOD）用于鲁棒HRV估计。2SR结合FOD和20个超像素区域在PR估计中达到最佳MAE（1.92 bpm），CHROM结合Lp范数被推荐用于HRV估计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 驾驶模拟器环境存在运动伪影等动态干扰，现有rPPG方法在此场景下的HRV估计精度不足，需要评估不同算法组合并引入新的信号处理方法来提升监测鲁棒性和准确性。

Method: 将四种rPPG算法（2SR、CHROM、POS、PCA）应用于基于超像素分割的面部区域（10和20个区域），并引入两种峰值增强方法——基于Lp范数和分数阶导数（FOD）；采用20秒片段的信噪比质量评估作为数据清洗机制以减轻运动伪影。

Result: 29名受试者在基线和驾驶模拟条件下，2SR+FOD+20超像素配置实现PR的MAE最低为1.92 bpm（sd=1.72）；SDNN的最佳MAE达0.061 s，RMSSD为0.081 s；心跳间隔检测F1得分为0.93；最优参数为Lp范数p=6-7，分数阶导数阶数1.0-1.4。

Conclusion: 推荐2SR用于脉搏率估计，CHROM用于HRV估计且使用Lp范数和20个超像素；使用FOD时需谨慎因其可能导致rPPG波形缓慢变化。

Original Abstract: This study evaluates remote Photopletismography (rPPG) algorithms, Spatial Subspace Rotation (2SR), Chrominance-based method (CHROM), Plane-Orthogonal-to-Skin (POS), and Principal Component Analysis (PCA), applied to selected superpixel-based facial regions (with target counts of 10 and 20 regions) for monitoring in a driving simulator. Two novel peak enhancement approaches, based on the Lp norm and Fractional-Order Derivative (FOD), are introduced to enable robust Heart Rate Variability (HRV) estimation. A signal-to-noise ratio-based quality assessment of 20 s segments serves as a data cleaning mechanism to mitigate motion artifacts inherent to dynamic recording conditions. In a sample of 29 participants recorded during baseline and driving simulation conditions, Pulse Rate (PR) is calculated with clinically acceptable accuracy across configurations (validated against simultaneous Electrocardiography (ECG) recordings), achieving the lowest Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 1.92 bpm (sd = 1.72) using 2SR with FOD and 20 superpixel regions. The best-case MAE reached 0.061 s for Standard Deviation of Normal-to-Normal intervals (SDNN) and 0.081 s for Root Mean Square of Successive Differences (RMSSD), with inter-beat interval detection yielding an F1 score of 0.93. Optimal parameters clustered around p = 6-7 for Lp norm and fractional orders of 1.0-1.4.
