However, CSI estimation in DAB+ is challenging due to the differentially encoded communication symbols across time. A wrong symbol transition estimation leads to a persistent multiplicative error in the sequential CSI sequence within a DAB+ frame. This paper formulates single-stream DAB+ passive radar as a posterior-probability-aware differential CSI tracking problem. The proposed method uses the previously tracked CSI as a channel prior, performs prediction-aided maximum a posteriori detection of current symbol, converts posterior transition reliability into observation uncertainty, and applies linear minimum mean squared error fusion to obtain a stable tracking CSI. A reliability-informed CSI fusion strategy is also introduced to preserve weak target information. Theoretical analysis is provided, showing guaranteed performance again in symbol and CSI estimation. Simulation results show that the proposed method can reduce CSI estimation error by over 15~dB compared with prior art. It also improves median target-to-background ratio by more than 11~dB in random fading scenes. Experiments in Sydney, Australia demonstrate improved range-Doppler maps for commercial aircraft sensing.

中文摘要翻译: 数字音频广播增强版(DAB+)因其提供持续性、高功率且地理覆盖广泛的甚高频(VHF)正交频分复用(OFDM)信号，成为无源雷达中极具吸引力的照射源。信道状态信息(CSI)感知方法可将单路接收的DAB+流转换为用于雷达感知的CSI序列，避免了传统无源雷达中需要单独接收参考信号的问题。然而，由于DAB+中通信符号在时间上采用差分编码，CSI估计面临挑战——符号转换的误估计会在DAB+帧内对序贯CSI序列造成持续的乘性误差。本文将单流DAB+无源雷达建模为后验概率感知的差分CSI跟踪问题。所提方法利用先前跟踪的CSI作为信道先验，进行预测辅助的当前符号最大后验检测，将后验转换可靠性转换为观测不确定性，并应用线性最小均方误差融合以获得稳定的跟踪CSI。此外，还引入了一种可靠性感知的CSI融合策略以保留弱目标信息。理论分析表明，该方法在符号和CSI估计方面具有可保证的性能。仿真结果显示，与现有技术相比，所提方法可将CSI估计误差降低超过15 dB，在随机衰落场景中中值目标背景比提升超过11 dB。在澳大利亚悉尼进行的实验证明了该方法在商用飞机感知中改进了距离-多普勒图。

</details>


### [236] [Time Segmented Beamforming via Dynamic Programming: Theory and Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24825)
*Manan Mittal,Ryan M. Corey,Diego Cuji,John R. Buck,Andrew C. Singer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时间分段无畸变响应波束形成器，通过数据驱动的时域分割自适应调整样本协方差矩阵估计窗口，以应对动态声学环境中时变干扰源对传统Capon波束形成器性能的退化问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统Capon波束形成器在实际应用中依赖批量样本协方差矩阵估计，这隐含假设批内数据平稳。在非平稳环境中，移动干扰源会"模糊"协方差矩阵，削弱波束形成器的零陷能力。现有固定或过长窗口的平均方法失效，亟需一种能自适应跟踪时变干扰源的方法。

Method: 受分段最小二乘法启发，该框架将数据驱动的时间分割引入Capon波束形成：在最小化输出功率的同时，动态调整SCM估计窗口以适应当地平稳性，并通过惩罚过度分割来防止过拟合，从而实现对时变干扰源的自适应跟踪。

Result: 论文未在摘要中给出具体数值结果，但理论上证明了所提方法在非平稳环境下能有效避免传统批处理方法中因移动干扰源导致的协方差矩阵"模糊"问题，保持波束形成器的零陷能力。

Conclusion: 该时间分段无畸变响应波束形成器为跟踪时变干扰源提供了一种原则性方法，通过在数据驱动的时间分割下自适应调整SCM估计窗口，在非平稳声学环境中实现了优于传统固定窗口Capon波束形成器的性能。

Original Abstract: In dynamic acoustic environments with time-varying interferers, effective beamforming requires identifying stationary regions over time. The Capon beamformer, a whitened matched filter constrained to maintain unity gain in the desired direction, theoretically relies on the instantaneous ensemble covariance matrix. Practical implementations rely on the batch Capon (or Sample Matrix Inversion), which estimates the sample covariance matrix (SCM) by averaging over a block of snapshots. This practical approach implicitly assumes that the data within the batch window is stationary and can be coherently combined. In non-stationary settings, a batch approach that averages over fixed or excessively long windows fails, as moving interferers smear the SCM and degrade the beamformer's nulling capabilities. To address this, this paper introduces a temporally segmented distortionless response beamformer. Inspired by the segmented least squares method, which fits piecewise polynomials to data while penalizing excessive segmentation to prevent overfitting, the framework extends practical Capon beamforming by incorporating data-driven temporal segmentation. This formulation minimizes output power while dynamically adapting the SCM estimation windows to local stationarity, offering a principled approach to tracking time-varying interferers.

中文摘要翻译: 在具有时变干扰源的动态声学环境中，有效的波束形成需要在时间上识别平稳区域。Capon波束形成器作为一种白化匹配滤波器，约束在期望方向上保持单位增益，理论上依赖于瞬时集合协方差矩阵。实际实现依赖于批量Capon（或称样本矩阵求逆），通过对一组快照块进行平均来估计样本协方差矩阵（SCM）。这种实用方法隐含假设批处理窗口内的数据是平稳的且可以相干合并。在非平稳环境中，对固定或过长窗口进行平均的批处理方法会失效，因为移动干扰源会模糊SCM，从而削弱波束形成器的零陷能力。针对这一问题，本文引入了一种时间分段无畸变响应波束形成器。受分段最小二乘法的启发——该方法通过拟合分段多项式到数据，同时惩罚过度分割以防止过拟合——本框架通过引入数据驱动的时间分割扩展了实用的Capon波束形成。该方法在最小化输出功率的同时，动态调整SCM估计窗口以适应局部平稳性，为跟踪时变干扰源提供了一种原则性方法。

</details>


### [237] [Lattice Modulo Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24974)
*Yhonatan Kvich,Yonina C. Eldar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于格理论的模数采样框架，用于多维带限信号。通过使用具有更小归一化二阶矩的格（如六边形格和E8格），相比传统超立方体格，能够显著降低重建均方误差（MSE），并在二维和八维信号上分别实现了16.7%和57%的误差降低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准模数转换器（ADC）将信号逐分量折叠到方形域，将恢复问题简化为独立的一维问题。本文旨在将该框架推广到任意格，以利用更高维度格在二阶矩上的优势，从而降低重建误差。

Method: 提出一个基于格理论的模数采样统一框架，将信号折叠推广到任意格；将现有恢复算法扩展到高维格设置；利用具有更小归一化二阶矩的格（如六边形格、E8格）来降低折叠信号功率和量化误差；从拓扑学角度将每种折叠几何解释为一个表面，其亏格反映格的复杂度。

Result: 在相同内半径下，二维六边形格相比方形格降低MSE 16.7%；八维E8格在加性噪声和量化噪声下均实现57%的误差降低。所需采样率与标准逐分量模数设置相同。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于格的模数采样框架在保持相同采样率的前提下，通过选择更优的格结构能够显著降低重建MSE，且高维格（如E8格）增益更大。该框架还具有通过比较器电路实现的硬件可行性。

Original Abstract: We propose a lattice-theoretic framework for modulo sampling of multidimensional bandlimited signals. Standard modulo analog-to-digital converters (ADCs) fold the signal component-wise into a square domain, reducing the recovery problem to independent one-dimensional problems. We extend the recovery guarantees to any lattice, requiring the same sampling rate as in the standard component-wise modulo setting. We also extend existing recovery algorithms to the general highdimensional lattice setting. Selecting a lattice with a smaller normalized second moment reduces the reconstruction mean squared error (MSE) through two complementary mechanisms: it lowers the folded signal power, which reduces the absolute noise energy at a fixed signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), and it reduces the quantization error when a matched lattice quantizer is applied. Higher-dimensional lattices offer better second moment compared to the hypercube lattice, with gains that grow substantially with dimension. Instantiating the framework in two dimensions with the hexagonal lattice reduces the MSE relative to the square at the same inradius by 16.7%. Furthermore, simulations on 8-dimensional signals using the E8 lattice to achive 57% in both additive and quantization noise. A topological interpretation connects each folding geometry to a surface whose genus reflects the lattice complexity, and reveals a natural hardware implementation via comparator circuits.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种基于格理论的模数采样框架，用于多维带限信号的模数转换。标准模数转换器（ADC）将信号逐分量折叠到方形域内，从而将恢复问题简化为独立的一维问题。我们将恢复保证推广到任意格，所需采样率与标准逐分量模数设置相同。我们还将现有恢复算法扩展到一般的高维格设置。选择具有较小归一化二阶矩的格，可通过两种互补机制降低重建均方误差（MSE）：一方面降低折叠信号功率，从而在固定信噪比（SNR）下减小绝对噪声能量；另一方面，当应用匹配的格量化器时，可降低量化误差。与超立方体格相比，高维格具有更好的二阶矩，且增益随维度显著增长。在二维场景中实例化该框架，六边形格相比在相同内半径下的方形格，MSE降低了16.7%。此外，在使用E8格的八维信号仿真中，加性噪声和量化噪声下均实现了57%的误差降低。一种拓扑学解释将每种折叠几何对应到一个表面，其亏格反映了格的复杂度，并揭示了通过比较器电路实现的自然硬件实现方案。

</details>


### [238] [A Unified KLD Framework for Duplexity and Deployment Paradigms in Cell-Free mMIMO-ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25018)
*Yousef Kloob,Mohammad Al-Jarrah,Emad Alsusa*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一分析框架，用于比较分布式无小区大规模MIMO（CF-mMIMO）集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统中不同部署方式（分离/共享）与双工模式（半双工/全双工）的性能，并利用KL散度统一衡量通信与雷达性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究缺乏一个统一的理论框架来系统比较CF-mMIMO ISAC系统中不同部署架构（分离/共享AP）和双工模式（HD/FD）之间的性能差异，尤其是在考虑实际硬件损伤（如残余自干扰、信道估计误差和杂波）的情况下。

Method: 建立了一个统一的分析框架，分析了四种配置（分离/共享AP部署 × HD/FD操作），考虑了残余自干扰、不完美干扰消除和杂波等实际损伤；利用Kullback-Leibler散度（KLD）作为统一度量，并基于广义似然比检验（GLRT）推导了将KLD与检测概率关联的闭式表达式；通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证。

Result: 全双工（FD）操作在半双工（HD）上实现了显著增益，前提是保持足够的自干扰抑制和干扰消除质量；共享部署通过更大的有效孔径增强了雷达性能，但通信-雷达耦合比分离部署更强。

Conclusion: 研究结果为下一代CF-mMIMO ISAC系统建立了部署指南和定量设计阈值。

Original Abstract: This paper develops a unifying analytical framework for comparing deployment and duplexing paradigms in distributed cell-free massive multiple-input multiple-output (CF-mMIMO) integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems. The system comprises distributed access points (APs) serving multiple downlink and uplink users while simultaneously detecting radar targets. Four configurations are analysed - separated and shared AP deployment under half-duplex (HD) and full-duplex (FD) operation, each incorporating realistic impairments: residual self-interference (SI) from transmit-receive leakage, imperfect interference cancellation due to channel estimation errors, and clutter. Kullback-Leibler divergence (KLD) is applied to serve as a unified measure, enabling direct comparison of communication and radar performance on a common scale. A generalised likelihood ratio test (GLRT) framework is developed to produce closed-form expressions linking KLD to detection probability. Monte Carlo simulations are used to verify our expressions, which demonstrate that FD operation achieves substantial gains over HD, provided sufficient SI suppression and IC quality are maintained, while preserving strong radar detection. It is also shown that shared deployment enhances radar performance via a larger effective aperture but exhibits tighter communication-radar coupling than separated deployment. These results establish deployment guidelines and quantitative design thresholds for next-generation CF-mMIMO ISAC systems.

中文摘要翻译: 本文开发了一个统一的分析框架，用于比较分布式无小区大规模多输入多输出（CF-mMIMO）集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统中的部署与双工范式。该系统由分布式接入点（AP）组成，在服务多个下行和上行用户的同时检测雷达目标。分析了四种配置——半双工（HD）和全双工（FD）操作下的分离与共享AP部署，每种配置均考虑了实际损伤：来自收发泄漏的残余自干扰（SI）、信道估计误差导致的不完美干扰消除以及杂波。采用Kullback-Leibler散度（KLD）作为统一度量，能够在共同尺度上直接比较通信与雷达性能。基于广义似然比检验（GLRT）框架推导了将KLD与检测概率关联的闭式表达式。蒙特卡洛仿真验证了我们的表达式，结果表明：在保持足够的SI抑制和干扰消除质量的前提下，FD操作相比HD实现了显著增益，同时保持了强大的雷达检测能力。还表明，共享部署通过更大的有效孔径增强了雷达性能，但其通信-雷达耦合比分离部署更强。这些结果为下一代CF-mMIMO ISAC系统建立了部署指南和定量设计阈值。

</details>


### [239] [A 100 GHz Wideband Reconfigurable Intelligent Surface Based on Orthogonal Polarization and Sub-Array Partitioning Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25138)
*Ruiqi Wang,Behrooz Makki,Atif Shamim*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种基于PCB工艺的宽带回波智能表面，工作于100 GHz附近，通过正交极化缝隙耦合贴片结构与子阵划分，结合AlGaAs SP3T裸片开关，实现了86–106 GHz范围内的波束重构，增益提升约5–10 dB，功耗仅0.165 W，验证了亚太赫兹频段低成本RIS方案的可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 亚太赫兹频段（~100 GHz）虽能提供超大带宽和超高数据速率，但存在严重的传播损耗和遮挡问题，覆盖受限。可重构智能表面（RIS）可动态调控电磁波传播以增强覆盖，但基于标准PCB技术在100 GHz附近实现RIS面临 fabrication限制、开关可用性有限、开关尺寸相对于单元过大、寄生效应显著以及控制复杂度高等挑战，亟需一种低成本、可扩展且实用的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种正交极化缝隙耦合贴片结构（orthogonal-polarization slot-coupled patch structure）与子阵划分（subarray partitioning）相结合的PCB基RIS设计。通过子阵划分减轻开关寄生效应、减少所需RF开关数量并简化控制架构。可重构性通过优化键合线互连（bond-wire interconnections）集成AlGaAs SP3T裸片开关实现。设计了六个子阵（每子阵4×4单元）对应不同波束角度，并制作了12×8单元的原型进行实验表征。

Result: 实测结果显示：在86–100 GHz频段内增益提升约10 dB，在100–106 GHz频段内增益提升约5 dB，同时保持0.165 W的低功耗。验证了宽带回波PCB基RIS在亚太赫兹无线系统中的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文成功展示了一种工作在100 GHz附近的宽带回波PCB基可重构智能表面，通过创新的正交极化缝隙耦合贴片结构与子阵划分设计，有效解决了高频段开关寄生、控制复杂等问题，实现了良好的宽频带增益提升和极低功耗，为亚太赫兹频段无线通信的覆盖增强提供了实用的低成本工程方案。

Original Abstract: The sub-terahertz frequency band offers extremely large bandwidth and enables ultra-high data rates for future wireless applications. However, severe propagation loss and blockage significantly limit coverage at these frequencies. Reconfigurable intelligent surfaces can dynamically shape EM wave propagation and provide a promising solution for coverage enhancement. Realizing such surfaces using standard printed circuit board technology is attractive due to its low cost and scalability, but it remains challenging around 100 GHz because of fabrication limits, limited switch availability, large switch size compared with the unit cell, switch parasitic effects, and high control complexity. In this work, we demonstrate a wideband PCB-based reconfigurable intelligent surface operating around 100 GHz. The design combines an orthogonal-polarization slot-coupled patch structure with subarray partitioning to mitigate switch-induced parasitic effects, reduce the required number of RF switches, and simplify the control architecture. The reconfigurability is achieved using AlGaAs SP3T bare-die switches integrated through optimized bond-wire interconnections. For proof of concept, a six-subarray structure with 4 by 4 elements per subarray is designed for different beamforming angles, and a 12 by 8 prototype is fabricated and experimentally characterized. The measured results show a gain enhancement of about 10 dB from 86 to 100 GHz and about 5 dB from 100 to 106 GHz, while maintaining a low power consumption of 0.165 W. These results validate the feasibility of practical wideband PCB-based reconfigurable intelligent surfaces for sub-terahertz wireless systems.

中文摘要翻译: 亚太赫兹频段拥有极大的带宽，能够为未来无线应用提供超高速率。然而，严重的传播损耗和遮挡显著限制了该频段的覆盖范围。可重构智能表面能够动态调控电磁波传播，为增强覆盖提供了一种有前景的解决方案。利用标准印刷电路板技术实现此类表面因其低成本和可扩展性而具有吸引力，但在100 GHz附近仍面临挑战，其原因包括制造限制、可用开关有限、开关尺寸相对于单元过大、开关寄生效应以及高控制复杂度等。本文展示了一种工作于100 GHz附近的宽带回波PCB基可重构智能表面。该设计将正交极化缝隙耦合贴片结构与子阵划分相结合，以减轻开关引起的寄生效应、减少所需射频开关数量并简化控制架构。可重构性通过优化的键合线互连集成AlGaAs SP3T裸片开关实现。为验证概念，设计了六个子阵（每子阵4×4单元）对应不同波束角度，并制作了12×8单元的原型进行实验表征。实测结果显示：在86–100 GHz频段内增益提升约10 dB，在100–106 GHz频段内增益提升约5 dB，同时保持0.165 W的低功耗。这些结果验证了面向亚太赫兹无线系统的实用宽带回波PCB基可重构智能表面的可行性。

</details>


### [240] [An Extended Object Poisson Multi-Bernoulli Filter with Zero-Inflated Poisson Model Using Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25316)
*Yuxuan Xia,Xueqi Qiu,Hyowon Kim,Chaoqun Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于粒子置信传播（BP）的扩展对象泊松多伯努利（PMB）滤波器高效实现方法，采用零膨胀泊松（ZIP）观测模型，通过因子图分解和局部一致性约束替代全局假设，显著提升了估计精度和运行效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于采样的扩展对象PMB滤波器在ZIP模型下的实现存在计算复杂度高、全局假设约束隐式处理困难的问题，亟需一种更高效且精度更高的实现方法。

Method: 1) 构建包含对象检测变量和双重数据关联变量（面向对象和面向测量）的因子化联合后验分布；2) 用局部一致性因子替代隐式的高阶全局假设约束，得到适合BP的因子图；3) 粒子实现中，未检测对象的泊松强度采用解析形式，检测到的伯努利分量的单对象密度用粒子表示。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法在估计精度和运行时间两方面均优于现有基于采样的扩展对象PMB滤波器ZIP模型实现。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于粒子BP的扩展对象PMB滤波器在ZIP模型下能够高效且精确地处理空测量集问题，是一种优于现有采样方法的方案。

Original Abstract: This paper presents an efficient implementation of the extended object Poisson multi-Bernoulli (PMB) filter under the zero-inflated Poisson (ZIP) object measurement model using particle belief propagation (BP). The ZIP measurement model separates a Bernoulli object detection event from the conditional Poisson generation of object measurements, enabling principled handling of empty measurement sets. Building upon the PMB mixture posterior, we present a factorized joint posterior over set of objects with object detection variables and a dual representation of data association using both object-oriented and measurement-oriented association variables. Notably, this representation replaces the implicit high-order global hypothesis constraint by local consistency factors, yielding a factor graph amenable to BP. In addition, we present a particle-based implementation, in which the Poisson intensity for undetected objects is analytic, whereas the single object densities of Bernoulli components for the detected objects are represented using particles. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method has superior performance than existing sampling-based implementations of extended object PMB filter with ZIP model in terms of both estimation accuracy and runtime.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种在零膨胀泊松（ZIP）对象测量模型下，使用粒子置信传播（BP）实现的扩展对象泊松多伯努利（PMB）滤波器的高效方法。ZIP测量模型将伯努利对象检测事件与对象测量的条件泊松生成过程分离，从而能够原则性地处理空测量集问题。在PMB混合后验的基础上，我们构建了一个因子化的联合后验分布，其中包含对象检测变量以及双重数据关联表示（同时使用面向对象和面向测量的关联变量）。值得注意的是，这种表示用局部一致性因子替代了隐式的高阶全局假设约束，从而得到一个适用于BP的因子图。此外，我们提出了一种基于粒子的实现方法，其中未检测对象的泊松强度采用解析形式，而检测到的伯努利分量的单对象密度则使用粒子表示。仿真结果表明，在估计精度和运行时间方面，所提出的方法均优于现有基于采样的扩展对象PMB滤波器ZIP模型实现。

</details>


### [241] [Deep Machine Learning in MIMO Communication Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25458)
*Mohammad Reza Ghavidel Aghdam,Alireza Naghavi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自编码器的端到端MIMO通信系统优化方法，通过联合优化发射机、接收机和信道，在瑞利衰落条件下显著降低了误码率，性能优于传统分块处理方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于分块处理的MIMO通信系统在噪声和信道衰落条件下性能受限，难以实现全局最优。本文旨在利用深度学习自编码器框架，实现发射机、接收机和信道的联合优化，以最小化误码率，提升无线通信的可靠性和效率。

Method: 将瑞利衰落信道嵌入自编码器框架，端到端联合训练发射机、接收机和信道模型；针对深度学习驱动的MIMO通信设计了专门的优化流程。

Result: 在不同信噪比（SNR）水平下的仿真结果表明，所提出的端到端系统相比传统分块处理方法实现了显著更低的误码率。

Conclusion: 提出的基于自编码器的端到端MIMO通信系统在无线信道条件下能够实现更高效、更可靠的通信，展现出优于传统方法的潜力。

Original Abstract: This paper presents an innovative approach to enhancing machine learning based communication systems, specifically focusing on multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) configurations using autoencoders. We optimize the transmitter, receiver, and channel simultaneously under conditions of noise and channel fading, aiming to minimize the bit error rate (BER). By incorporating the Rayleigh fading channel a widely recognized model for wireless channel impairments into the autoencoder framework, we directly train the communication system to handle real world conditions. We introduce a novel optimization process tailored for deep learning-based MIMO communication, and thoroughly analyze the resulting BER performance across various signal to noise ratio (SNR) levels. Our simulation results reveal that the proposed end-to-end wireless communication system achieves significantly lower BER compared to conventional block-based processing methods, highlighting its potential for more efficient and reliable wireless communication.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种创新方法，用于增强基于机器学习的通信系统，特别聚焦于使用自编码器的多输入多输出（MIMO）配置。我们在噪声和信道衰落条件下同时优化发射机、接收机和信道，旨在最小化误码率（BER）。通过将瑞利衰落信道——一种广泛认可的无线信道损伤模型——融入自编码器框架，我们直接训练通信系统以应对真实世界条件。我们引入了一种专为基于深度学习的MIMO通信设计的新型优化流程，并深入分析了在不同信噪比（SNR）水平下的BER性能。仿真结果表明，所提出的端到端无线通信系统相比传统基于分块的处理方法实现了显著更低的误码率，突显了其在实现更高效、更可靠的无线通信方面的潜力。

</details>


### [242] [Projected multi-reference alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25533)
*Amnon Balanov,Josh Katz,Tamir Bendory,Dan Edidin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了投影多参考对齐（MRA）模型中信号恢复的理论与方法，证明了在高噪声条件下，投影观测的前三阶矩足以确定信号的二面体轨道，并提出了基于矩的构造性恢复方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 受结构生物学应用启发，研究投影MRA模型——信号经随机循环移位和固定投影后通过噪声观测，目标是从丢失了方向信息的投影观测中恢复信号的二面体轨道。

Method: 在傅里叶-余弦坐标系下进行矩分析：一阶矩确定均值分量，二阶矩确定傅里叶幅度，三阶矩确定二面体双谱中的余弦相位耦合关系，从而实现从三阶矩到信号的构造性恢复。

Result: 理论证明前三阶矩可确定一般二面体轨道；实验表明在高噪声下，EM和直接矩优化的样本复杂度符合 n ≳ σ⁶ 的标度律。

Conclusion: 投影MRA中，通过前三阶矩可恢复信号二面体轨道，且EM和直接矩优化方法在高噪声下具有与理论一致的样本复杂度。

Original Abstract: Motivated by structural biology applications, we study the projected multi-reference alignment (MRA) model, in which an unknown signal is observed through noisy samples, each generated by applying a random cyclic shift followed by a fixed projection. The projection merges reflection-symmetric index pairs, thereby discarding orientation information. The goal is to recover the dihedral orbit of the signal. We prove that in the high-noise regime, the first three moments of the projected observations determine a generic dihedral orbit. The main mechanism is a reduction, at the moment level, from projected MRA to the reflection-invariant phase-coupling structure of dihedral MRA. In Fourier-cosine coordinates adapted to the projection, the first moment determines the mean component, the second moment determines the Fourier magnitudes, and selected third moments yield the cosine phase-coupling relations appearing in the dihedral bispectrum. These relations lead to a constructive recovery scheme from moments up to order three. We complement the population theory with finite-sample experiments comparing expectation--maximization (EM), direct moment optimization, and direct Fourier-cosine moment optimization. The results show that, in the high-noise regime, both EM and direct moment optimization are consistent with the predicted third-moment sample-complexity scaling $n \gtrsim σ^6$, where $n$ is the number of observations and $σ^2$ is the noise variance.

中文摘要翻译: 受结构生物学应用启发，本文研究了投影多参考对齐（MRA）模型，其中未知信号通过噪声样本被观测，每个样本由随机循环移位后施加固定投影生成。该投影合并了反射对称的索引对，从而丢弃了方向信息。目标是恢复信号的二面体轨道。我们证明，在高噪声条件下，投影观测的前三阶矩可确定一般的二面体轨道。主要机制是在矩层面将投影MRA约简为二面体MRA的反射不变相位耦合结构。在适应投影的傅里叶-余弦坐标系中，一阶矩确定均值分量，二阶矩确定傅里叶幅度，选定的三阶矩给出二面体双谱中出现的余弦相位耦合关系。这些关系导出了基于三阶矩的构造性恢复方案。我们通过有限样本实验对人口理论进行补充，比较了期望最大化（EM）、直接矩优化和直接傅里叶-余弦矩优化。结果表明，在高噪声条件下，EM和直接矩优化均与预测的三阶矩样本复杂度标度 n ≳ σ⁶ 一致，其中 n 为观测样本数，σ² 为噪声方差。

</details>


### [243] [Time-Varying Parametric Channel Estimation With CP Decomposition Tensor Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25593)
*Enrique T. R. Pinto,André L. F. de Almeida,Markku Juntti*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于CP分解和张量处理的快速参数化信道估计算法，用于时变频率选择性信道，性能接近多起点SAGE基准但计算成本低一个数量级。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 参数化信道估计（PCE）在6G ISAC中至关重要，但其计算复杂度远高于传统信道估计，亟需开发低复杂度方案。

Method: 基于规范多元分解（CP分解）和张量处理，结合ESPRIT初始化、分量精炼和精确线搜索交替坐标下降，提出全数字和混合接收架构两种变体。

Result: 所提方法明显优于基于CP的基线方法，性能接近多起点SAGE基准，但执行时间缩短约一个数量级。

Conclusion: 所提出的快速PCE算法在显著降低计算复杂度的同时保持了高精度估计性能，适用于6G ISAC系统。

Original Abstract: Integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) is a key use case for sixth-generation (6G) wireless systems, where parametric channel estimation (PCE) plays a central role in enabling sensing, localization, and channel equalization in high-mobility scenarios. However, PCE is typically more computationally demanding than conventional channel estimation, which motivates the development of lower-complexity solutions. In this letter, we propose a fast PCE algorithm for time-varying and frequency-selective (TVFS) channels based on canonical polyadic (CP) decomposition and tensor processing, combined with ESPRIT-based initialization, component refinement, and exact line-search alternating coordinate descent. Two variants are presented: one for fully digital and another for hybrid receiver architectures. Numerical results show that the proposed method clearly outperforms a related CP-based baseline while achieving estimation performance close to a multiple-start SAGE benchmark at a substantially lower computational cost, with about one order of magnitude shorter execution time.

中文摘要翻译: 集成感知与通信（ISAC）是第六代（6G）无线系统的关键用例，其中参数化信道估计（PCE）在高移动性场景中实现感知、定位和信道均衡方面发挥着核心作用。然而，PCE通常比传统信道估计计算需求更高，这推动了低复杂度解决方案的开发。本文针对时变频率选择性（TVFS）信道，提出了一种基于规范多元分解（CP分解）和张量处理，并结合ESPRIT初始化、分量精炼和精确线搜索交替坐标下降的快速PCE算法。提出了两种变体：一种用于全数字接收架构，另一种用于混合接收架构。数值结果表明，所提方法明显优于相关的基于CP的基线方法，且其估计性能接近多起点SAGE基准，而计算成本显著降低，执行时间缩短约一个数量级。

</details>


### [244] [Robust Quantum-MUSIC for DoA Estimation Using Rydberg Atomic Receiver Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25688)
*Sourav Banerjee,Neel Kanth Kundu,Prajwalita Borah*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出Robust Quantum-MUSIC (RobQMUSIC)框架，用ℓ1范数替代ℓ2范数进行相位恢复，结合迭代重加权最小二乘(IRLS)方法，实现存在异常值干扰时仍保持稳健的到达角(DoA)估计，且不增加算法结构复杂度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的Quantum-MUSIC算法采用ℓ2范数进行相位恢复，对硬件故障、传感器饱和或对抗性干扰产生的异常值测量高度敏感，导致估计性能严重下降。

Method: 提出RobQMUSIC框架，将ℓ2范数相位恢复替换为ℓ1范数加权相位恢复问题，并在交替最小化循环中嵌入迭代重加权最小二乘(IRLS)方案进行高效求解，不增加基线算法的结构复杂度。

Result: 在理想条件下，RobQMUSIC与Quantum-MUSIC的DoA估计精度几乎相同；在多种异常值污染水平下，当Quantum-MUSIC完全失效时，RobQMUSIC仍保持稳健的性能。

Conclusion: RobQMUSIC在保持与基线算法相近的理想性能的同时，显著增强了对抗异常值的鲁棒性，为量子无线感知在实际部署中面临的硬件缺陷和干扰问题提供了有效解决方案。

Original Abstract: Quantum wireless sensing using Rydberg atomic receivers enables high-sensitivity signal acquisition direction-of-arrival (DoA) estimation. However, it suffers from a fundamental limitation, where only the magnitude of the received signal is observable. The recently proposed Quantum-MUSIC algorithm addresses this problem by recovering phase information through alternating minimization and subsequently applying the MUSIC algorithm for DoA estimation. However, the existing approach relies on an $\ell_2$-norm phase retrieval step, making it highly sensitive to outlier measurements produced by hardware faults, sensor saturation, or adversarial interference. In this letter, we propose a \emph{Robust Quantum-MUSIC} (RobQMUSIC) framework that replaces the $\ell_2$-norm with an $\ell_1$-norm formulation. The resulting weighted phase-retrieval problem is solved efficiently via an Iteratively Reweighted Least Squares (IRLS) scheme embedded within the alternating minimization loop, requiring no increase in structural complexity relative to the baseline algorithm. Simulation results demonstrate that RobQMUSIC achieves near-identical DoA estimation accuracy to Quantum-MUSIC under ideal conditions, while maintaining robust performance over a wide range of outlier contamination levels at which Quantum-MUSIC fails entirely.

中文摘要翻译: 利用里德伯原子接收器的量子无线传感能够实现高灵敏度的信号采集及到达角（DoA）估计。然而，该技术存在一个根本性局限：仅有接收信号的幅度是可观测的。近期提出的Quantum-MUSIC算法通过交替最小化恢复相位信息，进而应用MUSIC算法进行DoA估计，解决了这一问题。然而，现有方法依赖于ℓ2范数相位恢复步骤，使其对硬件故障、传感器饱和或对抗性干扰产生的异常值测量高度敏感。本文提出了一种鲁棒Quantum-MUSIC（RobQMUSIC）框架，用ℓ1范数公式替代ℓ2范数。由此产生的加权相位恢复问题通过嵌入交替最小化循环内的迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）方案高效求解，且不增加相对于基线算法的结构复杂度。仿真结果表明，在理想条件下RobQMUSIC与Quantum-MUSIC的DoA估计精度几乎相同，同时在Quantum-MUSIC完全失效的多种异常值污染水平下，RobQMUSIC仍能保持稳健的性能。

</details>


### [245] [Blind SNR Estimation for FSO Communication Systems with Deep Fading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26063)
*Humberto V. Q. Melo,Robson A. Colares,Darli A. A. Mello*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文评估了M2M4和EVM两种方法在深衰落FSO通信系统中进行实时信噪比估计的性能，实验表明M2M4估计器能够可靠跟踪SNR变化，适用于触发收发器自适应。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 针对自由空间光（FSO）通信系统在深衰落条件下实时信噪比估计的需求，评估不同方法的适用性。

Method: 采用受控深衰落的实验装置，对比评估M2M4（二阶四阶矩）和EVM（误差矢量幅度）两种实时SNR估计方法。

Result: M2M4估计器能够可靠地跟踪深衰落FSO系统中的SNR变化曲线，性能优于EVM方法。

Conclusion: M2M4方法适合作为深衰落FSO通信系统中触发收发器自适应的实时SNR估计方案。

Original Abstract: We evaluate the M2M4 and EVM methods for real-time SNR estimation in FSO communication systems subject to deep fading. Using an experimental setup with controlled deep fading, we show that the M2M4 estimator reliably tracks the SNR profile, making it suitable for triggering transceiver adaptation.

中文摘要翻译: 我们评估了M2M4和EVM两种方法在深衰落自由空间光（FSO）通信系统中进行实时信噪比估计的性能。通过使用可控深衰落的实验装置，我们证明M2M4估计器能够可靠地跟踪SNR变化曲线，使其适用于触发收发器自适应。

</details>
